把大模型塞进聊天框并不能解决复杂问题。本文通过量化投资系统与 Minecraft 建造引擎两个案例，拆解一套面向 Agent 的工作流设计范式：领域 DSL、IR/DAG、验证契约与修复循环。

欢迎来四喜丸子做客！这是我们的家庭博客。在这里我们记录自己在生活中遇到的问题，分享解决问题的心得，希望帮看到这些的你省下时间少走弯路。我们会写各种各样我们想得到的内容：怀孕、养小孩子、听 podcast、买东西的省钱法。有共鸣、想法或者建议的话，[请毫不犹豫地来告诉我们](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#四喜丸子的联系方式)。因为我们可以一起讨论有意思的问题和解决办法，而且更重要的是你的鼓励会让我们把四喜丸子继续进行下去。

## 四喜丸子的登场人物 [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e5%9b%9b%e5%96%9c%e4%b8%b8%e5%ad%90%e7%9a%84%e7%99%bb%e5%9c%ba%e4%ba%ba%e7%89%a9)

### [Miki](https://fourhappylions.com/authors/miki/) [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#mikihahahugoshortcode1s1hbhb)

有些小聪明，但是生性好吃懒做，所以总是想办法优化身边的事情，想要让身上的负担越少越好的女性。平时以社恐形象示人，但是也有热心的一面。伴侣是 Colin。

### [Colin](https://fourhappylions.com/authors/colin/) [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#colinhahahugoshortcode1s2hbhb)

为人善良，兢兢业业做各种事情的男性。不轻易作出承诺，但是作出了就会努力做到。内心强大，但常常被各种似是而非的奇谈怪论带跑。伴侣是 Miki。

### ~~Baby~~ ~~Toddler~~ ~~Preschooler~~ School-ager (代号 Mk-I[1](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#fn:1)) [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#baby-toddler-preschooler-school-ager-%e4%bb%a3%e5%8f%b7-mk-i1)

~~Miki 和 Colin 的小孩，目前处于婴儿状态。喜欢研究身边世界的种种，心里不装事情所以很少有烦恼。此外性格不明。~~

~~Miki 和 Colin 的小孩，目前处于学步儿状态。喜欢研究身边世界的种种，新鲜人和事会观察很久很久。白天的时候很像在靠电池供电，一旦精力耗尽进入睡眠状态，就怎么也叫不起来。曾经是：Baby。~~

~~Miki 和 Colin 的小孩，目前处于学前儿童状态。智商和情商都集火在给自己搞到更多零食的观察型[NewType](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/Newtype)。憧憬着成为所谓的 “big kid”。曾经是：Toddler。~~

Miki 和 Colin 的小孩，目前处于小学生状态。自称在学校 “doing my best”。喜欢开包一周以内的文具，做手工，以及拿 freebies。因为 “too hard” 而不想成为大人。

### ~~Baby~~ Toddler (代号 Mk-II[1](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#fn:1)) [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#baby-toddler-%e4%bb%a3%e5%8f%b7-mk-ii1)

~~Miki 和 Colin 的第二个小孩，目前处于婴儿状态。不喜欢自己挪动自己（？）。性格暂时不明。~~

Miki 和 Colin 的第二个小孩，目前处于学步儿状态。喜欢被抱，唱歌，自言自语，以及模仿 Mk-I 正在做的一切事情并未经同意拿走 Mk-I 的东西。害怕家里扫地机器人，大概是觉得自己打不过。曾经是：Baby。

## 四喜丸子在谈的话题 [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e5%9b%9b%e5%96%9c%e4%b8%b8%e5%ad%90%e5%9c%a8%e8%b0%88%e7%9a%84%e8%af%9d%e9%a2%98)

  * [好物推荐](https://fourhappylions.com/categories/%E5%A5%BD%E7%89%A9%E6%8E%A8%E8%8D%90) (4)

  * [自己动手](https://fourhappylions.com/categories/%E8%87%AA%E5%B7%B1%E5%8A%A8%E6%89%8B) (7) 

## 四喜丸子的联系方式 [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e5%9b%9b%e5%96%9c%e4%b8%b8%e5%ad%90%e7%9a%84%e8%81%94%e7%b3%bb%e6%96%b9%e5%bc%8f)

✉️ [4happylions@gmail.com](mailto:4happylions@gmail.com)

## 四喜丸子的 FAQ [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e5%9b%9b%e5%96%9c%e4%b8%b8%e5%ad%90%e7%9a%84-faq)

### 为什么叫四喜丸子？ [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e4%b8%ba%e4%bb%80%e4%b9%88%e5%8f%ab%e5%9b%9b%e5%96%9c%e4%b8%b8%e5%ad%90)

Colin：有一次突发奇想了关于装饰的一个问题：相比于通常的字画，能不能在墙上挂上一些菜名的书法作品呢？想象一下，客厅沙发后面的墙上就是装裱起来的“梅菜扣肉”四个字草书——不过好像略有些餐馆的油烟气。那有没有名字不那么出戏的菜名呢？于是想到了“四喜丸子”。

Miki：因为这道菜好好吃。。。😋

### 为什么域名是 fourhappylions.com？ [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e4%b8%ba%e4%bb%80%e4%b9%88%e5%9f%9f%e5%90%8d%e6%98%af-fourhappylionscom)

可以参看[这篇文章](https://fourhappylions.com/posts/namesilo-domain/)。

### 为什么有广告？ [#](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#%e4%b8%ba%e4%bb%80%e4%b9%88%e6%9c%89%e5%b9%bf%e5%91%8a)

其实除了广告，我们还有亚马逊购物联盟 (Amazon Associates Program) 的链接——如果有人点击链接完成购物，我们会得到一笔很小的佣金，但对购买价格没有影响。我们将此作为写博客的一个小激励。每次看到一笔购买，都让我们意识到自己分享了有用的东西。希望这些不会影响阅读体验。广告只放在页面的最下方，购物联盟链接其本身应该也不会对阅读产生什么影响，有时其自带的图片还有益于展示物品。

* * *

  1. 代号出处在[这里](https://fourhappylions.com/posts/my-pregnancy-story-2-part-1/#week-13-day-6)。 [↩︎](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#fnref:1) [↩︎](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/about/#fnref1:1)

这里是四喜丸子的朋友们的博客。在私人社交平台垄断个人发言的今天，为了找回自己的空间、说自己想说的话，我们又一次捡起了十多年前就过气了的博客和RSS。本页的友情链接也是 Miki 为了 make blogging cool again 的一个小小努力。其实我们一直在这个广漠的宇宙里寻找新的朋友，所以加友情链接的话请[发邮件给四喜丸子](https://fourhappylions.com/pages/friends/4happylions@gmail.com)，或者直接在任何[支持 ActivityPub 的微博客社交软件](https://fediverse.party/)上联系 [Miki](https://fedi.fourhappylions.com/miki)。

  * [猫和柴的野游](https://meowshiba.com/): 两个人的旅游博客。不管是网站还是照片都美到呼吸快停了。
  * [椒盐豆豉](https://blog.douchi.space/): 一个行动力超高、心态又超好的友邻的博客。
  * [Noodlehead](https://noodlehead.life/): 工作上面又厉害又可爱的人，所写的房子博客。
  * [配信中](https://www.yocson.com/): 文笔很是我的菜，读起来很让人感动的博客。
  * [沉默之沙](https://yukieyun.net/): 多才多艺的友邻展现写作/生活中多姿多彩一面的博客。
  * [Albus’ Pensieve](http://pensieve.wangxindi.org/): 学术型友邻记录美术和音乐的艺术博客。
  * [无竹斋](http://bamboobone9.com/): 吃喝玩乐都在思考的的生活型博客。
  * [梦呓醒删](https://crescendomeow.wordpress.com/): 文艺型新友邻记录生活随笔的博客。
  * [June | 六月](https://shiorireads.ca/): 宅腐并且经营播客的友邻的日常生活博客。

众所周知, SpaceEngineers 的服务端(vanilla, 非 torch)上那个查看服务器内游戏信息的玩意是通过网络来获取信息的(localhost). 实际上这个东西有独立版本应用程序, 也在服务端根目录下, 叫做 VRageRemoteClient . VRage 是 SpaceEngineers 使用的游戏引擎, 这个游戏引擎本身也是 Keen 开发的.

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"79f82afae797e25a","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/linux/shi-yong-vnc-lai-yuan-cheng-guan-li-fu-wu-qi.html","title":"使用 VNC 来远程管理服务器","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":234,"body_sha256":"1c2951b0678511039ce653869de23d40e9f309d884f61d2ff83ab192db4ee364"}
-->
众所周知, VNC 可以让用户远程访问服务器的图形界面, 这对于一些工作站来说是非常棒的功能. 但是网上能搜索到的资料可谓是讲不清道不楚, 现在我来教你如何最简单的使用 VNC.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"c9d9975e7d6aab51","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/linux/linux-xia-wu-xian-shi-yong-jetbrains.html","title":"Linux 下无限试用 JetBrains","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":157,"body_sha256":"5d3db2641ed22126ec7a410c5512f5150a5b770101624f7af5d20f23867b32b7"}
-->
致命警告: 目前的 JetBrains IDEA 2021.2.3 版本(2021-10 月)需要联网并登录账号才能试用, 试用信息保存在账号内, 本文已失效.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ce87abb7a50c2d50","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/windows/shi-yong-kms-ji-huo-windows.html","title":"使用 KMS 激活 Windows","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":212,"body_sha256":"1cb9289edad376944fb252eed155c0fc312013c16e92f02b458438f039176677"}
-->
在很多情况下, 出于不可抗力, 必须使用 Windows 系统, 于是如何激活就成了一个问题. 特别是在虚拟机中使用 Windows, 个人用户不可能为每个虚拟机都购买一次授权.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"476f277b832a0e8f","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/xu-ni-hua/wei-kvm-zhong-de-windows-xu-ni-ji-qi-yong-virtio.html","title":"为 KVM 中的 Windows 虚拟机启用 VirtIO","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":421,"body_sha256":"324e1d1b5caeb4da07469406f83464b56760086c733369f43fa91665e17656da"}
-->
众所周知, KVM 全称 Kernel-based Virtual Machine, 很显然这是为 Linux 设计的, 那么 Windows 客户机在其上的表现果不其然非常稀烂, 尤其是硬盘 IO 性能低至不可忍受(当使用 qcow2 时). 这时候就必须要用 VirtIO, 它是一套为半虚拟化设计的通过一组标准化的 API 来实现在多种不同的虚拟化方案中复用专用的客户机驱动代码的解决方案.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

-->

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"73da08567961631f","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/yun-kai-fa/gcp-pubsub-push-zhi-iap-bao-hu-de-appengine.html","title":"GCP Pub/Sub push 至 IAP 保护的 Endpoint","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":338,"body_sha256":"b2462e5fb716018643e95237e167f6f8d21a94cc980b18fb2344183d992ef659"}
-->
众所周知 IAP(Identity Aware Proxy)对 AppEngine 开启后, 所有 GAE 的 service 都需要先通过 OAuth2 鉴权才能访问. 普通用户在浏览器打开 IAP 保护的 GAE 地址就会被跳转到登录页面去登录谷歌账号. 那问题来了, Pub/Sub 的消息如果要推送到一个 IAP 保护的地址应该怎么操作.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6e01c6fb872fc594","feed_id":"hiczp.com-92526dd57b3b","feed_url":"https://www.hiczp.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://www.hiczp.com","feed_title":"czp's blog","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.hiczp.com/jia-mi-huo-bi/wa-jue-men-luo-bi.html","title":"挖掘门罗币","author":"czp3009@gmail.com","published":"2026-06-14T04:49:19Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":287,"body_sha256":"fdbe7c5c29c7b105c966a8a2962a7448e80860bf929b2452d83f7d5ad43f84cb"}
-->
Monero(中文名 门罗币, 缩写 XMR) 是一种故意设计成只有通用计算芯片才能挖掘的币, 目的是对抗 ASIC 制造商以阻止少部分人垄断大部分算力.与其他大多数用显卡才能有效挖掘的加密货币不同, XMR 只有用 CPU 才能有效挖掘.

Welcome to my personal website! I am glad to hear what brings you here. Do not hesitate to share in the comment below!

# Who am I? [#](https://jdhao.github.io/about/#who-am-i)

Hi, I am jdhao, a machine learning engineer who is passionate about working with computer vision. I am experienced in tools like NumPy, [OpenCV](https://jdhao.github.io/tags/OpenCV/), and [PIL](https://jdhao.github.io/tags/PIL/), and also in data visualization tools like [Matplotlib](https://jdhao.github.io/tags/Matplotlib/). I have broad experiences working on various vision-related projects, ranging from image retrieval, OCR, document image analysis and reconstruction, to video deduplication.

## 添加友链 

建这个博客十多年来，互联网在不断发展但我曾经友链的存活数却在急剧减少，如果时至今日你还在坚持使用**博客** 写文章，且不涉及商业、弹窗广告，都欢迎交换友链。

但是仍需要你的网站满足下面的最基本的要求：

  1. 提供HTTPS访问且为主流浏览器信任的证书
  2. 不要连续宕机或证书过期超过1个月
  3. 不要使用免费顶级或非顶级域名，如 `.tk` `.ga` `.cf`
  4. 不使用免费PHP空间（Shared-Hosting空间)

## 本站信息 

  * 名称： Cysime Moflu
  * 简介： 再不会遇见第二个时光
  * 网址： <https://blog.cysi.me>
  * 图片： [请右键或长按复制图片链接](https://image.glaceon.net/uploads/202205012353016.jpg)
  * 横幅： [请右键或长按复制横幅链接](https://image.glaceon.net/2022/06/08/62a05e4097cab.png) （如果你的友链模板是卡片式的，需要一张图片作为背景横幅）

## 友链动态 

欢迎访问我的 [友链屋](https://blogroll.cysi.me/) 查看我友链们的动态。

[The RC 4000 Multiprogramming System ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RC_4000_multiprogramming_system) is historically notable for being the first attempt to break down an operating system into a group of interacting programs communicating via a message passing kernel. RC 4000 was not widely used, but was highly influential, sparking the microkernel concept that dominated operating system research through the 1970s and 1980s. (wikipedia)

{"v":1,"guid":"0e4538ce3577d220","feed_id":"puming.zone-8361bf4a2f0c","feed_url":"https://puming.zone/index.xml","site_url":"https://puming.zone","feed_title":"浦明的博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://puming.zone/top/about/","title":"","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":1558,"body_sha256":"01375268a002f3b601071377fd78a5873678a0a4ae2f8924624df9837484ca6d"}
-->
博主目前就职于绿盟科技星云实验室高级安全研究员岗位，base西安，在云原生应用安全及安全创新领域有丰富的实战经验，长期从事Kubernetes、微服务、服务网格安全、无服务器安全的研究。在绿盟科技任职期间，参与过安全应用商店、安全编排与自动化响应(SOAR)平台、容器安全平台、安全产品(如WAF)云原生化的架构设计和研发工作。作为核心作者参与2018年《绿盟科技容器安全技术报告》、2021年《绿盟科技云原生安全技术报告》、2021年《CSA CSTR 云原生安全标准》等编写，联合著有《云原生安全：攻防实践与体系构建》一书。 个人专利 《一种编排微场景剧本的方法、服务器、介质及计算机设备》已公开 CN111835768A 《一种微服务应用场景下的安全防护方法与装置》已公开 202111259722.5 《一种云原生应用安全场景下的全流量劫持方法、装置和介质》官方提交 2023110749701 《一种服务网格场景下的入侵检测方法与装置》申请公开 CN112153049A 参与专利 《一种用于处理安全事件的方法、装置、介质》 已公开 CN111835768A 《一种安全应用交付方法、装置以及存储介质》已公开 CN109710270A 《一种延时任务处理方法及装置》 已公开 CN202010236020 《一种竞态条件漏洞利用的检测方法及装置》已公开 CN202010806212 《一种容器环境异常检测的方法、装置、介质及计算机设备》申请公开 CN111813497A

写在我的博客PV10万时：聊聊编程与画画。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a8118f922441d758","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/101","title":"如何在Python程序中使用配置文件.ini(ConfigParser)","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":181,"body_sha256":"f8649c20040915310bbce81b1a77b8224b8c84449b7f7d1114fa6b7320c0d17a"}
-->
在Python程序的编写中，我们经常要为程序配置一个配置文件，有了配置文件，可以记录一些应用的参数配置，方便下次使用时直接调用。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1a68b032ee725121","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/103","title":"基于Django构建的小学生英文单词背写检测考试系统。","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":318,"body_sha256":"d2a85c3df5c1901a4f30b2b022842e74d263068a83ef6660cf05f6917f149702"}
-->
孩子的学习总是让父母担忧，英语学习也是让家长的头疼之事，做为程序员老爸不能整天忙于编程而忽视了孩子的教育，我们也要搞点事情。孩子英文单词到底背的怎么样了？别怕，用PyWordTest,创建题库，轻松搞定孩子的英语单词拼写情况。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1bb2f398cca9a88c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/106","title":"碎言静态博客","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":135,"body_sha256":"65fdc939c6e31de5e14c2fb420db830e780a985661d8d0cae3d9776befc69c7d"}
-->
碎言这个名称取自与"碎言片语"，码兄累了吗？累了就休息一下，为未来的自己留下一些碎言片语吧。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"5c15e9e04a67df99","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/11","title":"Python 采集数据三步曲之[Requests + re.py模块进行数据采集]","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":221,"body_sha256":"e1d8e2ee7a5ecc34aed8bd0ff9bb914e64455534715ce1f2482ff174c6260042"}
-->
当我们熟悉了re和Requests两个模块，就可以开始愉快的数据采集之旅了，我们先为自己找一些需要采集的数据，比如股票价格数据、新闻或是美女图片等，只要你喜欢。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"f76d835db3193c43","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/12","title":"Python os.path模块中一些常用方法的整理总结","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":194,"body_sha256":"f004bccc2ae9034921c03c604f018fa215cae00150428e252e34ab2124a2363b"}
-->
跟计算机打交道，和目录与文件接触应该是最多的了，所以，掌握Python中的目录与文件操作是非常重要的，本节主要介绍os.path模块的一些常用方法
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"b3bb8f2d912b2923","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/13","title":"Python open()函数文件打开、读、写操作总结","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":199,"body_sha256":"5396d9b44899f95900987cd0e4a74ddb3df1e0566283c62f9429b617c081fe9e"}
-->
在编程和计算机的使用中，与文件打交道是必不可少的，每天都要打开读取写入很多的文件，所以了解Python的文件打开、读、写操作是非常有必要的。

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

.ini 文件是Initialization File的缩写，即初始化文件，是windows的系统配置文件所采用的存储格式，统管windows的各项配置，一般用户就用windows提供的各项图形化管理界面就可实现相同的配置了。但在某些情况，还是要直接编辑ini才方便，一般只有很熟悉windows才能去直接编辑。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

装饰器（decorator）是干嘛的？对于受到封装的原函数来说，装饰器能够在那个函数执行前或者执行后分别运行一些代码，使得可以再装饰器里面访问并修改原函数的参数以及返回值，以实现约束定义、调试程序、注册函数等目标。装饰器一般返回一个包装器（wrapper），而functools.wraps就是装饰包装器的装饰器。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

上次我说这话的时候还是2022-09-23，一下子都过去大半年了！花了三天的时间把之前写的静态博客重构了，这次的重构还算满意挺开心的，重构达到了预期的效果。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2588d74c516dc0cd","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230404163916","title":"Pico.css大道至简返璞归真\u002d\u002d一个简单轻量化的CSS框架","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":363,"body_sha256":"db3a7f20ae3343b46a2a72bf2d4facbea21081ce232b7e6b28555266f3ee80e2"}
-->
Pico.css是一个简单轻量化的CSS UI框架，最大的特点是样式都基于HTML原始的标签名和内置的属性，少用甚至是不用class来定义样式，写出来的代码语义清晰，可维护性强，能够帮助开发者构建自己的UI系统，也可以直接用于快速的小型项目中。Pico.css在Github上已经有高达8.8KStar。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"789f998f4e880e10","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230405202140","title":"程序员的痛苦：颈椎病和肩周炎","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":493,"body_sha256":"e87b9a7883e42074d5f63a29544e1b0066cdd069ad238abbc852935ae9f04dfa"}
-->
我想提醒所有程序员兄弟姐妹们，要保护好自己的颈椎和肩膀。长时间保持同一姿势容易导致肌肉疲劳和紧张，从而引起颈椎病和肩周炎。因此，我们应该每隔一段时间就起身活动一下身体，适当地放松自己的颈椎和肩膀。此外，我们还可以采取一些预防措施，例如使用合适的电脑屏幕高度、定期锻炼颈部和肩部的肌肉等。让我们一起保护好自己的身体，更高效地工作！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a2682143b9a4c903","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230408093536","title":"free_blog 碎言静态博客主题","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":156,"body_sha256":"04adcbab456569c0063c37162541a5d80435375b64ad3cc3fa3e5351623d5c15"}
-->
大道至简，返璞归之，这是一款侧重与内容的博客主题,本项目为[碎言静态博客](https://github.com/bosichong/suiyan.git)主题。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"55a39c440255f9ba","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230410075954","title":"使用Python编写并部署个人静态博客:(一)分析与设计","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":226,"body_sha256":"3f4228fb3449bc7b7178761cc1e772210d106900f2c60ddf28942ecc5f67a1fa"}
-->
独立博客是指个人独立运营的博客，通常是由个人自己设计、开发、维护和更新的。相比于其他博客，独立博客可以自由定制，自制主题，自由评论不受平台限制等优点。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6d1d23b8efe6870b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230413095913","title":"2023重学前端:HTML CSS JavaScript基础复习","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":215,"body_sha256":"e46519ed6dcd66cef8e3f8d27e029dfd86be7fde844713056a2c156f5e8b30b4"}
-->
最近写了一些前端的项目使用了一些框架例，但是突然发现自己的前端基础是如此的渣，渣的自己好尴尬，遂决定重新学习并复习一下前端的HTML CSS JavaScript基础。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"c8a300f21d2e7aaa","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230416175852","title":"从回调函数到JavaScript异步编程","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":320,"body_sha256":"18147400f837a56f397279b4206aa5b27da5945aea66cdef088dc4b03cd2d792"}
-->
异步编程是JavaScript中的一个重要概念。为了理解异步编程，首先需要了解同步编程的工作原理。在同步编程中，代码按行执行，每行必须在下一行开始之前完成执行。当处理长时间运行的任务或需要等待外部资源时，这可能会导致性能问题。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6904cd4d44ca92ef","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230418122936","title":"css布局如何选择flex和grid?","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":242,"body_sha256":"1b02437f44b570cd9230d980faaac4ecf5014ff78a3d7d47d8d855008127b81f"}
-->
在前端界面的设计时，经常要用到css的布局属性，这里主要对flex、gird做一些学习上的总结，flex和grid都是CSS布局模块，允许响应式和灵活的设计。但是，它们具有不同的用例和属性。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"4523fe9a441b3f78","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230420101527","title":"使用Python编写并部署个人静态博客:(二)实现静态页面的输出","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":190,"body_sha256":"e84da595e2c9e990a08e9e487fcd187efdd822232e2a016e1a6ec665627ce9f5"}
-->
实现静态页面的输出其实很简单，按着顺序来就行：搜集Markdown文件和站点配置文件->组织数据并渲染模板(jinja2)->输出HTML并保存到静态目录中。

Vue (发音为 /vjuː/，类似 view) 是一款用于构建用户界面的 JavaScript 框架。而Element Plus是一款基于Vue3面向设计师和开发者的组件库
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"af5fee7d82a67ea4","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230504105514","title":"css的过渡效果transition的使用","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":146,"body_sha256":"67fe2bb95210b58f7bdcbdfcc7e1d2eb074ff76d2d1ecbdea1c9072d1945b936"}
-->
CSS的transition允许您对CSS属性的更改进行动画处理，这对于在网站上创建平滑和视觉上吸引人的效果非常有用。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"839c5a99e7fec7c2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230504201422","title":"CSS动画animation的简单应用","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":166,"body_sha256":"25d11b5b7599bb33788e5808389cf682b04601de69ff10d6f912dfec1ee1920c"}
-->
要在CSS中创建动画，我们可以使用animation属性。该属性允许我们指定动画的关键帧、持续时间、时间函数、延迟和迭代次数。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
css有很多神奇的效果都是使用CSS伪元素利用视觉差来制作的，以前没怎么深入的研究过css，这次复习css的知识点才恍然大悟，原来css这么cool。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

这阵子一直在读编程相关的工具书，枯燥乏味的很！昨天偶然间在哪里？不记得是哪里看到的有人推荐《写作是门手艺》这本书，赶紧去京东淘了一本，今天就收到了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"0d89d575d066536a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230509101659","title":"使用Fetch发送Ajax请求","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":229,"body_sha256":"71437b20994d15b4a244754a41c4d5c049892c03c0183547196bc989539d2df9"}
-->
很久以前，在JavaScript中发送请求或者获取资源的时候很多人都是用的是`jQuery`，直到最近一直使用`axios`，但有的时候不想使用第三方的请求的库，这个时候你可以试试Fetch。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

Tailwind CSS是一种和传统CSS编写方式不一样的开发模式，Tailwind CSS编写大量的css的class，预置超多的css样式，让开发者可以通过在HTML的class中以命名的方式去调用这些class，几乎可以不用去写style的代码，只使用class的名称来代替。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a85f8a1f42acf9b0","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230511191515","title":"安装了Tailwind CSS IntelliSense插件 vscode没有代码提示","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":193,"body_sha256":"5abcbeadcc2bac743e7834bdb5db97a52d9fcda1e319e33e52ffd84939a69e38"}
-->
今天在vscode中安装Tailwind CSS IntelliSense这个插件，但是安装完后，编写HTML中的class的时候并没有得到tailwindcss的代码提示，看来配置项设置有问题。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d7a1160d61cabde8","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230513082050","title":"再谈TailwindCSS的使用心得","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":209,"body_sha256":"1822d9fe46e088bf86474668950a2d2535c441315bb5a5b89fd35048b03592af"}
-->
TailwindCSS上手确实很好用，有点明显，但是坑也挺多的。本来打算使用TailwindCSS制作一个全新的博客主题，但是在实际的使用过程中有一些不爽细节不吐不快。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"bb4c9947bd67715e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230513104151","title":"display:none没有隐藏元素所占空间的原因","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":382,"body_sha256":"6dd0c33195dd077fcad90a7b0384aa7122baafe9553a52c2fd9b8a3bf5f79db4"}
-->
今天想写一段前端的效果，然后就手动使用原生JavaScript生成了一些HTML的DOM，其中有一些是带有`display:none;`隐藏的css属性的，我的本意是想隐藏这些元素并且不占用空间，但是使用JavaScript动态生成的DOM，动态添加的css属性`display:none;`的话，元素就会发生只隐藏但空间占用的情况还是存在。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"7e385f6aa2d0e0a0","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230514163231","title":"一些关于中文独立博客的聚合网站","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":328,"body_sha256":"af498ac8682b63e11c1d599a3b6dce7f77bcb892f4e528dc95aee3a940539cfd"}
-->
这阵子忙里偷闲的更新了一些博文，因为之前中间有段时间没有更新博客，所以对独立博客的这个圈子了解的也不是很多，最近通过一些独立博客的友情链接，发现了几个有意思的关于中文独立博客聚合的网址，觉得很有趣，不敢独享分享给大家。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"62cbc0ddf179b14b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230515105053","title":"fetch()如何读取请求中的`user-agent`？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":356,"body_sha256":"ef06dcb7bbaee1d0efb39761fea2f3ced916e4995d4213904ebdc06c85d80dc7"}
-->
但是添加后我想测试一下看看请求中headers是否添加了`fetch()如何读取请求中的`user-agent`？`,本来希望打印`response`中的herders来查看`user-agent`是否伪装成功，直接在fetch的response中遍历headers是无法获得`user-agent`这个属性。所以只好搭建一个临时服务器端来打印请求的request。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"8f4072daa69746cb","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230515215136","title":"async/await和fetch()的使用","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":275,"body_sha256":"804a90b56611dd7138973af64085a6755c71775de0a042e0522c935dc199886a"}
-->
async和await关键字让我们可以用一种更简洁的方式写出基于 Promise 的异步行为，而无需刻意地链式调用 promise。而FetchAPI可以通过网络访问资源，您可以发出HTTP请求（使用GET和POST其他方法）、下载和上传文件。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"9e285161955b8456","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230516132817","title":"使用Moment.js或Day.js对HTML页面上的时间进行格式化","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":331,"body_sha256":"19d362c908f3d74a4cbd8f56a816f7764a65f03c81c595b587beb7aed64e8bae"}
-->
一直想对博客页面的时间进行格式化，把标准的时间格式修改成XX时间前或相对时间，因为时间已经直接输出到了页面DOM中，所以考虑直接使用相关的js库来实现比较方便，搜了一下发现二个JavaScript的时间处理库比较不错，他们是：`Moment.js`和`Day.js`。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

这几天一直琢磨这给博客文章页面添加个第三方的评论系统，最好是免费的，然后就搜到了`giscus`，从安装到使用，用一个字来评价：真特么绝！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

博客的静态页面的生成都是由博客的基础数据支撑的，搜集博客的基本信息和博客文章数据，是生成博客静态文件的核心功能。

-->
许多年前曾经接触过Django，那时候还是1.X版本，现在都到了4.2版本了。今天抽空复习一下有关Django的相关技术要点。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a3eafedd9c527b40","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230520081153","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(二)编写和调试Model模型","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":335,"body_sha256":"e56e4e43ed48d75730bc8bfd25edbfef9332d372d8461ca9677bfe996de544a7"}
-->
对于web应用的编写应该从哪里开始呢？如果包含了数据的增删改查，那么对于使用Django开发web应用来说，从模型(Model)开始最好不过了。模型准确且唯一的描述了数据。它包含您储存的数据的重要字段和行为。一般来说，每一个模型都映射一张数据库表。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"98ab2dcb1fa60cde","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230522201116","title":"如何解决 Some chunks are larger than 500 KiB after minification？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":59,"body_sha256":"c02020a6e11756eaf3ce06e42aa5e5a5a1aaf25d5b2215c99874f0fde32278f9"}
-->
解决vite打包超出默认值，出现警告的错误。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"e3b0ed2b389d39d2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230522201800","title":"vite打包后element样式被其他框架样式覆盖的解决方法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":436,"body_sha256":"547193d38625a1e03b4e8614ad7e020c6b6ce87bbceb23d5bf865802d8b27acd"}
-->
今天打包一个项目，发现element button 样式被其他矿建的样式覆盖了，项目里还使用了`tailwindcss`，当时打包的时候提示文件大小超过了默认值，我就修改了vite build的配置项chunkSizeWarningLimit,并且使文件分开打包，然后就发现element的样式后边始终跟随一个样式表，会覆盖element button的背景颜色的样式，网上搜了很多方法都没有效果。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"543ce25e42852d9e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230524211340","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(三)Model模型字段","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":161,"body_sha256":"825b6d091617323e49d21d7f0846ee5966fa025e39f035cde4878544ec691351"}
-->
Django的ORM在Python中算是数一数二的，使用起来非常的简单和丝滑，文档也写的简单直观，相对于SQLAlchemy有过之而无不及。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"64125c52efe70e7e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230525084144","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(四)数据的查询Django的ORM","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":172,"body_sha256":"f172e3baece81a41959facf4611181791a444d930ed4d8e37814f53c916e4af2"}
-->
Django的ORM操作非常简答，几乎把所有你能想到的数据库的操作都封装了，即便没有你需要的，也可以使用原生的SQL语句进行查询。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"c7e492bc26461805","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230527070832","title":"css实现漂亮的彩虹渐变文本效果","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":144,"body_sha256":"81980c497337480195d67a54c26d42d938ca58bcc64babac1b48e18735a71240"}
-->
这段代码的效果是，为h1标签设置一个彩虹渐变的背景，并使文本颜色透明，从而实现彩虹渐变的文本效果。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"3bff8bcdb69912b2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230528074013","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(五)Django中常见的关系字段及其示例","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":307,"body_sha256":"1d7aa1cdeb7dbc6f32811394196628b603ef94356793266bc8470368830c69c2"}
-->
Django是一个使用Python编写的开源Web应用程序框架，它提供了一种方便的方式来处理数据库关系。Django的关系字段允许你在模型之间建立各种类型的关联关系，如一对一、一对多和多对多关系。以下是Django中常见的关系字段及其示例。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"baee3331680199de","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230528074712","title":"SQLAlchemy的关系字段和Django的关系字段他们的不同点。","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":173,"body_sha256":"0f05ce5f7806f26b3be1b9c0e07fd1cc34a6d7aa20ca847598619d11bfb84468"}
-->
SQLAlchemy和Django都是流行的Python框架，用于处理数据库关系。虽然它们都提供了关系字段的概念，但在实现和使用上有一些不同点。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"336904b799ffc9e3","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230529075925","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(六)Django的路由","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":298,"body_sha256":"e03450273991c2539f6e51369c55ec333835a2c04e08d982a0addfd606addde0"}
-->
在Django中，路由由URL模式和视图函数组成。URL模式定义了URL的结构和匹配规则，而视图函数则定义了当URL匹配时要执行的代码逻辑。Django的路由系统允许你根据URL的不同部分进行模式匹配，并从URL中提取参数传递给视图函数。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"7b11d9e2cb95e77f","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230529193749","title":"Vue+element+Vite安装 Tailwind CSS","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":51,"body_sha256":"9c46cdaf8c2bd1dca910b48f44259fc193c0029b1c22ce9ee5d9af17ad59290d"}
-->
在Vue+element+Vite项目中设置 Tailwind CSS。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"78b25909dabd3b9f","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230530145118","title":"JS中数组常用API总结，slice、splice、map、reduce、shift、filter、indexOf......","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":253,"body_sha256":"0e35142be76a6647b75f0301b6fb5eae07f06da5f4929b80b0fa43e1377c5eb1"}
-->
在前端开发中，数组是一种常见且重要的数据结构。数组提供了许多便捷的方法来操作和处理其中的数据。本文将简单介绍前端中数组常用的API，包括添加、删除、截取、合并、转换等操作。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"be6abfd3d1c7d08c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230531194542","title":"Tailwind CSS 相关的组件库","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":203,"body_sha256":"4715b8b094b776bf6340625009ecd4bf7a7fd5c81518546dec86f2f9e4834096"}
-->
Tailwind CSS 在任何特定于框架/技术的UI开发中都有发展趋势。我发现了一些最好的使用基于 tailwind CSS的组件库，您可以在其中直接使用tailwind自定义组件。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"14ab2aee53dca42c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230601090428","title":"记一台老机器MacBook Pro macOS 10.14 Mojiave升级到macOS Monterey 12.6.2","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":505,"body_sha256":"4b046036285fe29d121ecee4ea9b94676888ebf86de211f5d674411f47f21ddf"}
-->
我有一台老机器MacBook Pro(2015)的，最近有些软件提示如果系统不升级到macOS Monterey，软件将不再提供最新版的支持与升级了，之前升级过一回macOS Big Sur，体验非常糟糕，后来就退版本了退回了macOS Mojiave，这次我有忍不住升级了，但是这次是最后一次系统升级了，在之后的版本就不支持这个老机器了，所以豁出去了，还好升级后使用体验还不错，记录下来供有相同经历的友人参考。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"8d825549ed4880ec","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230602070849","title":"Python权限管理框架 Casbin PyCasbin 的简单操作","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":347,"body_sha256":"6cc05101869a7929cf7b335153169859c155486194b24535cf99ac574bf8f3f4"}
-->
大多数应用都会和权限与权限管理打交道,有的时候我们希望有一套拿来即用的权限管理框架,既可以在小型的个人应用中使用,又可以应付大型应用的复杂权限的管理,Casbin可以满足你的这个小小愿望.Casbin支持多种编程语言,今天我们来试试Casbin在python下的简单操作.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"21941ce9d44e220d","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230602101713","title":"macOS更新后 终端提示：The default interactive shell is now zsh.","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":114,"body_sha256":"2a88ebcfb2b74ef4397405193a84e0d66baa5cbc7f45561ba6309a41b76197fa"}
-->
昨天升级了macOS系统到macOS Monterey,之后每次打开终端都会有一些提示，真的好烦人啊。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"bb79b2858749f6bf","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230603073259","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(七)Django 模板(TEMPLATES)","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":242,"body_sha256":"ed7773d2fda17b2e0c390711805286eed628085ac40e5bfd40b88c1944b7525d"}
-->
作为一个网络框架，Django 需要一种方便的方式来动态生成 HTML，最常见的方法是依靠模板，一个模板包含了所需HTML输出的静态部分，以及一些特殊的语法，描述了如何插入动态内容。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d8a8332003a1f81c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230604084835","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(八)Django 模板语言语法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":380,"body_sha256":"a0c0badcf20f11f4285f730b81f77a5d97f3ab087ddb6118ba8133cfe806fbd5"}
-->
Django的页面渲染是通过Django的模板语法在模板中渲染上下文的变量来进行输出的，Django 的模板语言是为了在强大和简单之间取得平衡而设计的。它的设计让习惯于使用 HTML 的人感觉很舒服。如果你接触过其他基于文本的模板语言，如 Smarty 或 Jinja2 ，你应该会对 Django 的模板感到很舒服。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"91b54de9e258e203","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230604161547","title":"如何单独使用Django ORM 在非Django项目中使用Django ORM？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":375,"body_sha256":"6f8668930b59829e77d18ccc81560baf34bc0541fa541b75f02d6f245510d034"}
-->
在Python中进行一些不是很复杂的数据库操作的时候我们都会选择使用一些ORM框架，主要的ORM框架包括Django ORM、SQLAlchemy、Pony ORM、Peewee和Tortoise ORM等。这里Django ORM在使用方面更为简单和人性化。但是Django ORM和Django的绑定很紧密，如何单独使用Django ORM呢？在非Django项目中使用Django ORM？
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"0036bd9b34fbe7c3","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230605083417","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(九)使用Django的上下文处理器（context processor）","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":462,"body_sha256":"46d8751ab7043acde885541042690fd74aef3a3d93f6bb513ab13e1e7db8a62c"}
-->
web站点的上下文对于新手可能有些陌生和晦涩，这个东西有什么用？上下文就是一个变量或是函数，在整个web站点中都可以调用并获取其中定义的值。在Django中，全局上下文（Global Context）是指在每个视图中都可用的全局变量或函数，可以在模板中直接访问。Django提供了一种机制来定义全局上下文，使得这些变量或函数在所有的模板中都可以使用。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"fbda4f1dc0c56886","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230607211438","title":"Matrix 矩阵 JavaScript代码生成器","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":271,"body_sha256":"b4de89a82d952bcfa95f3c7feac22971ec8b65e98a9ebdae9616c92e9eb139b2"}
-->
今天写了个Matrix 矩阵 JavaScript代码生成器，其实几年前就用Python的pygame实现过一个Matrix，最近在玩JavaScript canvas，索性使用JavaScript原生代码又撸了一遍，并且做了个代码生成器，方便修改Matrix的一些参数。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"f4d51665970586a9","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230608102340","title":"CDN引用lodash报错 _.debounce is not a function","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":304,"body_sha256":"d38004225c54ad7c9405b99861aef12bf75499a856e9b216edebe4aa68038986"}
-->
今天有一段代码需要用到JavaScript的防抖功能，忽的想起lodash中有这个功能函数，由于代码比较简单，只需直接引用CDN即可，便在网上搜了CDN直接引入，但是在使用_.debounce方法的时候却报错`_.debounce is not a function`!真是奇了个怪了！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"feaf4f6f223f4f7b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230608184436","title":"一行代码升级你的node.js为最新稳定版","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":205,"body_sha256":"0c49e05a9c18ac5df29e417fbdfe18a98f3b48430edca081f756cfcd9a87e678"}
-->
今天，有一个应用需要更高版本的node.js，但是我从来都没有升级过node.js，特意Google了一下，发现一个超级简单的升级node.js的方法，这里简单的记录一下。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"df76bf08027e55db","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230608185126","title":"clipboard.js 一款简单好用的将文本复制到剪贴板的现代方法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":192,"body_sha256":"6403b1bd5a5cf7ab03ae0788303667b54a368bee7aa85af3b6dbccb0ccb2f2d5"}
-->
复制文本到剪贴板本应该不难。它不应该需要配置的繁琐步骤或大量的加载空间。最重要的是，它不应该依赖于Flash或任何臃肿的框架clipboard.js。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"f7c79864e304e190","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230609081842","title":"使用Python编写并部署个人静态博客:(四)逐步输出静态页面","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":442,"body_sha256":"71c8d56731ab0c9d4d9c8211b4fde7c4b33591611d17bf5c652a0949c0043f24"}
-->
使用Python编写并部署个人静态博客这个系列写第一篇的时候这是信心满满，以为会有很多同道中人，但是发现独立博客都挺少了，自己编写并部署博客的更是少的可怜。这就好比用电饭锅做饭，难道还要自己研究怎么做电饭锅？直接使用WordPress或一些静态站点生成器不好吗？嗯，挺好的，但是我还是选择自己编写并部署自己的博客。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"20d91674e86acac1","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230609094312","title":"好看的博客千篇一律 有趣的博主万里挑一","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":349,"body_sha256":"8093be72562f189ffe9564575935c6ba7da7ad162808d98a0bdaad5040e647f5"}
-->
每次看到有关独立博客的博文，都觉得这类博文比较沉闷悲观，大多都是阐述玩独立博客的人不多或是越来越少，充满着怀旧的复古气息，博主一般都会对自己从建站到现在是如何坚持过来的洋洋洒洒的写上一篇，嗯？咱就爱看这类的博文，一个博主真实的一面。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"99765f64002a8833","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230611084325","title":"Django 4.2 复习笔记(九)Django Forms 使用表单","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":244,"body_sha256":"06899820d2ba4d30a1dc57234cfc4988d36ab27f445286347549c6690cbc5b35"}
-->
在web日常中表单是必不可少的，处理数据的输入与交互在web站点中是非常重要的，Django提供了一系列的工具和库来帮助您构建表单来接收网站访客的输入，然后处理以及响应这些输入。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

对于静态博客来说，对于页面的批量生成和更新需要更快的速度才是最佳，这里推荐Python的异步协程文件处理方法，可以达到很不错的速度！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"9c400f88b21b1113","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230614151948","title":"你最讨厌什么样的独立博客？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":304,"body_sha256":"540f3bc48726f584ae30821eca43e88dd3c175eac0777f4065b0b2809b969706"}

搭建、部署、专心撰写博文，是每个博主都需要花费一些心思去做的事，每个博客就像一扇窗，我们透过窗口看到的那些光就是作者内心真实的表露，有些强烈如火焰，有些温柔如月光。但是有些，我真特么的想一脚给他踹关上。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

花了几个小时基于daisyUI Tailwind CSS做了一个博客主题，很早就想把博客的样式换成基于Tailwind CSS为基础框架的，今天终于实现了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"749ce6b14e9b6da9","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230617102523","title":"从一次浏览器兼容问题到何不放过自己做个快乐的博主","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":236,"body_sha256":"ac5500aad78622e176b66073749e5f476a6838ca236f33912a812a6f6d6c5d85"}

新主题做的很满意，主要是在于技术选型、策划、编写的过程中很畅快，让这个39°炎热的初夏有了一丝丝凉意，唯一美中不足的就是iPhone的Safari浏览器有个兼容性问题让人头疼。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d94aec78b4af1128","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20230726132925","title":"export exports module.exports 这三个命令的区别是什么如何使用？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":189,"body_sha256":"6387c9456d579efa3e5cb8b33e289eebc5e530f0034f6340291affb262fd142f"}
-->
export exports module.exports 这三个命令中export是是ECMAScript 6（ES6）中引入的模块化规范,exports module.exports是Node.js中用于实现CommonJS模块规范的对象。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

{"v":1,"guid":"de88c19509c33e5e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20240922204505","title":"Flux.1 入门必知：硬件、环境、模型","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":569,"body_sha256":"aab3a76fec5b13643497b418f0e860ea3f3d2eaee3c2b3ec317e7bcc2e15be91"}
-->
在当今的数字艺术领域，Flux.1 和 ComfyUI 已经成为了两个不可忽视的关键词。Flux.1 是由 Black Forest Labs 开发的一款先进的图像生成模型，以其出色的文本到图像的转换能力和高质量的图像输出而闻名。ComfyUI 则是一个基于节点的图形用户界面，专为 Stable Diffusion 设计，提供了高度的可定制性和模块化的工作流程。在本文及后续的文章中，我们将深入探讨 Flux.1 和 ComfyUI 的核心概念和功能，帮助您更好地理解这两个工具的强大之处。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

在当今的编程世界中，AI 辅助编程工具正逐渐成为开发者们的得力助手。这些工具不仅能够提高开发效率，还能为代码质量提供有力保障。本文将为大家介绍十款免费的 AI 辅助编程工具，包括它们的优缺点以及官方网址。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"5769a29a43516c15","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20241102083314","title":"使用 Next.js 和 Tailwind CSS 搭建静态图片展示站点并部署到 Vercel","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":398,"body_sha256":"90b3cbc9162f7d354b7f575a8e143a98445988caf9b5fec3c3fb1f56ef536d9c"}
-->
在本文中，我们将介绍如何使用 Next.js（纯.js 文件，不使用.jsx）和 Tailwind CSS 搭建一个静态图片展示站点。该站点将具有首页以瀑布流模式展示图片，点击图片可跳转到图片展示页面，所有图片信息存储在一个 JSON 文件中，并且为每个图片生成单独的静态页面。最后，我们将把这个站点部署到 Vercel。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
在编程的世界里，我们都在追求更高效、更优质的开发体验。今天，我要向大家介绍一款编程领域的神器——豆包旗下的 MarsCode。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ddedc5fa8288dcdb","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20241107085752","title":"PrimaryOralMathPack 小学生口算题生成器","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":443,"body_sha256":"7bf5fd6a98f5e8894b37fd9116f58abf16a682fc584a49621d319c1c45af096f"}
-->
PrimaryOralMathPack是一个功能强大的NPM包，专门用于生成小学生口算题。它可以根据用户指定的各种参数，如运算类型（加法、减法、乘法、除法）的属性、运算步数、题目数量、是否求结果或运算项、是否包含括号以及数值范围和运算符列表等，生成符合要求的口算题数组，为教育工作者或家长创建个性化的口算练习材料提供了便利。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2c59c1adaeda8210","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20241107092043","title":"npm login 和 npm publish 总是连接不上npm的网站的解决方法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":282,"body_sha256":"9f395a7a4ffe2100ab3b238a6691c04af2596e886273492ec5bc9fd7224107aa"}
-->
昨天像发布一个npm的包，但是，npm login 和 npm publish 总是连接不上npm的网站，一直显示超时，但是我ping 一下，是可以ping通的，但是就是连接不上，然后我就去网上查了一下，总结一下连接不上npm网站的解决方法。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"bcf26b4dae6b01b5","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20241110091751","title":"Node.js新手教程：使用3M（nvm, npm, nrm）","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":195,"body_sha256":"73fe8040715b6c6b9e80452c137827cdb615ada7cba3c1ff27d9103dc4380ea6"}
-->
本教程将带你了解如何使用3M工具（nvm, npm, nrm）来管理Node.js环境。这些工具将帮助你轻松切换Node.js版本，管理项目依赖，以及优化包的下载速度。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"5fc5b3237c98fd03","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20241114083651","title":"Socket.IO：实时通信的强大工具","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":235,"body_sha256":"67ded0bb38772d8fad0074d20c859bb85fd1dfdb8bb3ead5178e55e0fc91bb99"}
-->
Socket.IO 由 Guillermo Rauch 开发，旨在解决 WebSocket 在不同浏览器和网络环境中的兼容性问题。它提供了统一的 API，让开发者无需担心底层传输协议的差异，轻松实现实时双向通信。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

Heroicons 是一个免费的开源图标库，由Tailwind CSS的创始人开发。它提供了大量精美的 SVG 图标，旨在与 Tailwind CSS 无缝配合使用，但也可以在任何项目中使用。图标分为三种风格：Outline（轮廓）、Solid（实心）和 Mini（迷你），以满足不同的设计需求。网站提供了图标浏览、搜索和下载等功能，方便开发者和设计师快速找到并使用合适的图标。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"72e9d9df29cca333","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250117141522","title":"读《九宫格写作法》的一些感悟","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":277,"body_sha256":"56c60e461f961f949f677bfee1d0310d447c6cc2c626bce8fd12f57b82882cf0"}

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"8e5cf5133ce6aebc","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250118121259","title":"你有遗憾吗？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":169,"body_sha256":"fe464d905d0c76a9f7dfd71ad4ec5d8e51a36beef9883659cc1b81cc5b88de17"}
-->
我本以为，人生最大的遗憾是所爱隔山海，山海不可平，后来才知道，海有舟可渡，山有路可行所，山海皆可平，难平是人心。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"219f9435f5f17920","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250119001052","title":"一些提供网站安全在线检测的站点","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":364,"body_sha256":"0b9c508b1b336f1802663182047f8b66940932883ef42a88d03e4a312dab1040"}
-->
| 早上使用手机的微软浏览器点开了自己的博客，弹出了一个广告的窗口，很是郁闷，因为这个广告他只弹出一次，在手机上也无法查询这个弹窗的出现，刷新又不会再次出现。 不知网友们打开碎言是否有弹出广告页面？无论弹出与否请在页面下边留言告知与我，谢谢了！！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2f4cfe4c8cce0c4f","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250119120215","title":"读完《React进阶之路》闲聊几句","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":207,"body_sha256":"d3468c001c7c5cacac0349ef3e319a0333ce391b1bad11c698c11c68444796ce"}
-->
今天读完了《React进阶之路》，其实并没有完全读完整本书，只是把前半部分的基础理论和路由那部分仔细的看了看，后边的项目实战并没有仔细的去研究。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"7ee1e009069d5ae7","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250120132739","title":"阅读随感","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":261,"body_sha256":"d37c36700372f16fc987c7cb1bdacb43d4916e3e81c64da50bb89662f374a693"}
-->
今天在微信读书上啃《JavaScript语言精髓与编程实践》，这是一本极其深入详细讲解JavaScript基础理论的神书。它不同于我们平时在互联网上看到的那些教程，不愧是资深程序员大佬爱民老师的巨作。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1b688eb66364b2f0","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250121004101","title":"trae.ai 字节出品的AI IDE","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":167,"body_sha256":"83d4d49a5161fe562894bb3cbf5abdfe2d52609f9f009a544d69882f37ba30db"}
-->
昨天晚上在X上看到很多程序员大佬都在推荐一个全新的AI IDE：trae.ai 根据X上大佬们的评价对这款新的智能IDE还是充满推荐的。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"95736b5467990047","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250123005146","title":"让giscus的主题跟随blog的主题一起变化","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":659,"body_sha256":"4980c6cbf723d1b9bccc84e2c91424ed78208c2bebdc2abb58a303bbd37ebfaf"}
-->
之前一直在使用`giscus`作为blog的评论系统，个人评价`giscus`接入方便、使用简便，依托全世界最大的代码平台Github，其在评论系统商的功能可以说是非常的丰富和完善的，而且稳定可靠，不用操心。之前的blog只有一个主题，所以`giscus`也选择了对应的主题，后来blog增加明暗主题的切换，但是`giscus`主题却没有跟随系统的样式进行切换，当用户切换了blog的主题是，`giscus`的主题却没有切换，如果是明暗主题切换就会有很明显的对比，所以就有了下边的内容就是如何解决`giscus`的样式跟随主题切换。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"bccfb1754aa5a33d","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250124153309","title":"DeepSeek的一些使用感受","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":153,"body_sha256":"16bfd45854eef3f25c7944d564e7d3205d0fce11c2727127c564d6208d682a3d"}
-->
DeepSeek最近这几天可以说是很有风头，很多地方都报道了他的新的推理模型，抱着试试看的心态，也去体验了一下。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"c1bf27679240db49","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250127123717","title":"那些年前你说年后要做的事就不要再拖了。","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":88,"body_sha256":"195a008f50b04a873e3eb1a275b03d23c815a82789e72c06d9a2d861ff0ae87f"}
-->
时间总是在不经意间悄悄流逝，尤其是在春节前这个忙碌的时期。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"0491457c316e8c4b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250127161136","title":"DeepSeek线上服务受到大规模恶意攻击!","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":190,"body_sha256":"86abe761eb6f60d70e03ab731ba34898f64c8029c7b1afdd93553404cd9dd714"}
-->
近期DeepSeek线上服务受到大规模恶意攻击，为持续提供服务，暂时限制了+86手机号以外的注册方式，已注册用户可以正常登录，感谢理解和支持.
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"08aaac520767e4d8","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250201234913","title":"DeepSeek官方暂时恢复了访问","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":192,"body_sha256":"387ba901c49eff076642d915249b1790b9106bca85f7db803cd2f181b424f727"}
-->
从大年初一开始，DeepSeek官方的一些功能无法访问和使用，例如联网、API。今天早上测试了一下，联网的功能已经恢复了。而且API也可以使用了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"cb51a69e62581778","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250203045150","title":"在Windows下用Chatbox和Ollama部署DeepSeek-R1:14b","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":383,"body_sha256":"9ef7aeb072d74a544e0ca009063255fa90debd5dbca01e91cb1aa2bab7118838"}
-->
博在本地部署大模型的过程中，我曾尝试在Windows Docker下安装Open WebUI。然而，这个过程并不顺利。Docker的配置、模型的加载以及与GPU的适配等问题，让我遇到了不少坑。经过一番折腾后，我意识到，或许需要一种更简洁、更高效的方式来实现本地部署。于是，我转向了Chatbox和Ollama的组合。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1914126ee160e7ee","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250206133704","title":"马斯克：理想、勇气与「钢铁侠」精神","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":251,"body_sha256":"068af82746f3018d4a41ca024f343407543331046fd04fc1c88f7b0ad9c9d022"}
-->
花了整整12个小时，我终于读完了《硅谷钢铁侠：埃隆·马斯克的冒险人生》。读完后的第一感觉是，这本书在某些方面似乎只是开了个头，更像是人类殖民火星和探索未知宇宙任务的前传。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"906cf3a2e3136a55","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250209010356","title":"免费开源的AI图片放大神器：Upscayl","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":297,"body_sha256":"e550a1bd88a6c6f9852ab8bef41a83065243265b71878fb318f448869dab40fe"}
-->
Upscayl 是一个免费开源的 AI 图片放大工具，它可以将低分辨率的图片放大到高分辨率，同时保持图片的清晰度和细节。Upscayl 使用了深度学习技术，可以在放大图片的同时，自动调整图片的颜色和亮度，使其更加自然和真实。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"dec631ead641671e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250211004704","title":"一次 DeepSeek Kimi Gemini 代码编写能力的测评","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":175,"body_sha256":"98972db0df5aef35d6f1d5bb98df3b9cc186ac257acad5568090e11dc8b0f14a"}
-->
首先，这是一次偶然性的测评，并非专业的测评不具有代表性，仅从个人的使用感受如实记录而已，大家看看就好哈，不必太在意。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"facc46323d3d04e6","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250213140203","title":"《马云传》我的回忆和情怀","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":142,"body_sha256":"8d9da396e7a3e2103473507ed3dba900744ffe11afe715acafdfff3a610dd6dd"}
-->
从这本书的写作水准来说，我读完后给了一个「一般」的评分。这本书或许叫《阿里巴巴传》会更好一些。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6298ea8d6a8b3564","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250217124533","title":"trae支持Windows了,赶紧试试吧！","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":143,"body_sha256":"65d1ddff782b1b9f3ae37fab8a3a942758c4fc932871941e0c399eb3dd2ad6cd"}
-->
目前打开官网就可以下载Windows版本的trae了，而且不需要代理，不需要付费，chat模式、Builder模式均可使用！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"0c0a89294cbd519a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250221005728","title":"code : 无法将“code”项识别为 cmdlet、函数、脚本文件或可运行程序的名称。","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":159,"body_sha256":"083673229298531a0366c0c1372810d4c029322dc7439f281a5a13403cd05be4"}
-->
今天像往常一样运行了终端命令，正常来说会使用`code`命令在vscode中打开新建的md文件，但是今天运行了命令，报错了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6c44b9769d73a3a9","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250221140606","title":"MarsCode编程助手模型接入满血版DeepSeek R1/V3","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":132,"body_sha256":"963f5c40dc7d20eea69baf981e167a53cbec38fb6ff549305961c5b45119d2a5"}
-->
今天突然发现字节旗下的豆包变成助手也支持DeepSeek R1/V3了，这简直是太棒了，而且还是免费使用！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"04a4fb5c34713288","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250225235722","title":"域名续费前将域名转移到了Dynadot","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":207,"body_sha256":"651d0d84353957f7c3541cf0b209be5363bf22f1592c17b1d2390f4887ab6694"}
-->
昨天收到了邮件，提示suiyan.cc的域名即将到期，需要续费。早些时候就想把域名转移到Dynadot，但是一直没有行动，趁着这个机会，就把域名转移到了Dynadot。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"f1b5e2f078832c9d","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250302060242","title":"分享自己维护了12年的开源程序：BabyLog 宝贝日记","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":463,"body_sha256":"ba57df8f3b73e96d3945bfe7d8363075f4908e0c3bbe105b5e8c9976bde31961"}
-->
这个程序写在12年之前，当时和一些有孩子的亲属朋友聊天，他们总是说孩子几个月就会走路了，一岁就会背古诗了，但这明显就是在吹牛逼，让他们拿出一些证据却拿不出来。我想或许他们记得这些事，但是不记得具体的时间和细节，如果有一个可以记录这些的与孩子相关的日记，那会是一件很有意义的事。于是，我就开始编写这个程序：BabyLog。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d929d83bc6143e70","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250303132257","title":"国内首个 AI IDE:Trae 发布了","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":175,"body_sha256":"99a3e0ae923b08054a8066ca9441dfd1573a0068d3091ab3d840f8d7df9cea38"}
-->
Trae 是国内首个 AI IDE，深度理解中文开发场景。AI 高度集成于 IDE 环境之中，为你带来比 AI 插件更加流畅、准确、优质的开发体验。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"9a40869d364c7604","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250306041951","title":"辽西没有春天","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":349,"body_sha256":"52cda491413598fa390a7307c5f5148e78337f4e0d4534e4780ab60db9237cf5"}
-->
辽宁的西部是个奇怪的地方，春天这个季节在这里是非常的不明显的，气温一旦上来就是从零下十几度升温到零上二十几度，你可以体验那种一天四季的感觉。和人聊天说说天气，礼貌而又不失优雅，避免了尬聊的可能，而且，大家可以因为天气的问题聊上一会。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"fff10ce15810c7b4","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250317142354","title":"Windows下Ollama部署gemma3:12b","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":183,"body_sha256":"772e38971f43d0a578389f9b21bef431d2e3f0e715164485a49bab2870b45e83"}
-->
对比deepseek 14B,Gemma3 12b的回答更加快速，在某些问题的回答上也更加准确。当然，这也是我个人的见解，建议大家可以下载部署体验一下。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"4de7be1cead0c391","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250318131205","title":"没有信号的焦虑\u002d\u002d手机的SIM卡坏了","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":205,"body_sha256":"f15e7dfa610be91f727877ffe54dc8441dff265ed54f05dd962d48e1439cbe47"}
-->
下午开车出去，下车之后习惯性的拿出手机看了一眼，发现手机么信号，这就奇了个怪了！一种奇怪的焦虑逐渐的袭来，我猜，大概率是手机的SIM卡坏了？
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"412fc2f2daf21dfd","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250321131137","title":"Phaser3 学习笔记","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":443,"body_sha256":"786827db4363b78ccc86e55af4b0fad288173b34fa30f63b6ca5ba9c75d85569"}
-->
起因是一直在关注「極客死亡計劃」最近写的几篇博客，前几天他终于发布他的游戏大作「为战斗献上心脏！」看的心痒痒，又想写游戏了，为什么要说有又？因为以前懵懵的编写过一些小游戏，但是大多都是按着实际的案例照抄，主要是学习游戏的编写，具体自己完整的构思和制作并没有体验过，所以就有了这篇Phaser3的学习笔记。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"627c392e7f2e9582","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250322013314","title":"Phaser3 游戏开发环境搭建","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":233,"body_sha256":"b57f94f216be95644489e1cabaf011eff0c31d90c7f2ee57dbad1afec0eb4efc"}
-->
Phaser3的游戏开发环境搭建其实很简单的，如果你引入CDN的话，只需要一个HTML页面即可，但是这里我并不推荐使用CDN搭建，这里我推荐使用Vite。对于代码编写我推荐trae或vscode。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"013095a64c857438","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250323073926","title":"蠢事三则","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":55,"body_sha256":"d4e5ff72506a913932f9219be09c53ad310cae56c8dc9cbd37904ecd58db47f8"}
-->
有什么蠢事？说出来让大家开心一下吧！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"fa22776a366fdfc9","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250330051804","title":"突然想做一个模拟养成类的游戏","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":247,"body_sha256":"7e4b782acd04551d09002e16dc748b998b5e0261dc700800fe3ed7529e2dec3c"}
-->
前几天一直在各个游戏引擎的框架选择中飘忽不定，同时也在纠结和构思制作一个什么样的游戏，简单的在头脑中构思跑了一遍就觉得没意思了，太难的复杂的又觉得制作起来遥遥无期。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"9a560833b6d863ed","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250409233731","title":"游戏开发中的断离舍","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":279,"body_sha256":"0665932e5aa257f6ade570c63f9f0689eb6286653b836caa8e81fa823491b30a"}
-->
使用godot开发游戏的进度还在缓慢的推进中，本以为大部分时间会在绘制场景和制作动画上了，但是我确卡在了游戏的世界观设计上了，痛定思过，决定先把游戏的世界观设计好了，再开始绘制场景和制作动画。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"b2ae813cb5b770d5","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250421024909","title":"又到了流鼻涕流眼泪的季节了","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":208,"body_sha256":"d523a47e8e3815e9dd6447d64dfd31ad16a76f6ec4d8e320beccada62aee3ad2"}
-->
哎呀，每年春暖花开的时候，别人踏春享受春天快乐时光的时候，就是我的最「悲伤」的季节，那个痛苦也只有经历过的才会懂，鼻涕眼泪流的那个伤心啊！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d0ead00886bc7084","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250429145547","title":"中老年游戏:《暗黑破坏神III》国服技术测试现已开启","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":212,"body_sha256":"ee5f0df74b4574ab5701270fa5b6c04ecf5c2600c0edae7534ed7e2247e02dd7"}
-->
本来以为大萝卜3国服这辈子都不可能重启了，结果网易已经在4月16日正式开启了技术测试，前几天闲着没事就下载了这款养老的中老年经典游戏玩了一个星期。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2020710076df9092","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250509005137","title":"纠结的五月","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":263,"body_sha256":"47a2ae5b78c867d10ee2d842cd1ffc6273857d131f7b60cf163bb907f368aa99"}
-->
五四青年节的那天我带着孩子去了牛河梁遗址，整个五一假期都是刮风下雨的，刚好4号那天的天气真的很不错，就赶紧带着孩子去了牛河梁遗址博物馆，走的长深高速，高速上的车也是不多很通畅。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"671e873b5e0d9372","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250611233730","title":"做个游戏做到开始怀疑人生了","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":232,"body_sha256":"ba5ea92eded0641f9c3deb80daff0704ecd240bd88f9cd21a0c8c035a0b95a25"}
-->
一晃俩月过去了，我的游戏开发进度真是惨不忍睹，从信心满满的开始，到折磨的崩溃，再到开始怀疑人生。从做好一个游戏到把这个游戏做完，这真是一个自我折磨的过程。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"75bd6a78baac8669","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250704021309","title":"琐事不断的老年程序员","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":188,"body_sha256":"67284cf8b5f5f3f6fb9a299d8bd84fbd2dcde7b48f08abf4e04069b24ee06cc4"}
-->
都说程序员过了35岁就会被裁员，35岁确实是一个坎，这个年龄段大部分人都会结婚生子。我觉得影响程序员工作能力的主要原因就是：琐事。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"876f18484616e99b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250712144920","title":"抽象的理想和具体的现实","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":307,"body_sha256":"be4d81ef57d10bef9f0b2ebc5334cc8c6b6a6531835466c2f54d946c3db8320a"}
-->
最近一直在构思一些游戏的中的细节，有的时候想了几天都没有具体实现的方式，不过，大部分游戏的场景最终都还是找到了具体实现的方法。这期间，我的思绪不断地回到这个游戏构思的初期，那个抽象出来的理想中的游戏世界。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"af7e5cd4b818d2e7","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250724005122","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录01","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":351,"body_sha256":"135ca2e0061b74a031399633ab33c1319e5e3efa090a29672c2aed6bd24dc6cd"}
-->
游戏开发已经有段时日了，我决定在我的博客记录这个游戏开发过程，一方面是督促自己抓紧开发游戏的各个环节，记录游戏开发的过程和学习使用Godot开发游戏的经验，另一方面也是希望能够吸引到对这个游戏感兴趣的玩家，当然如果能够有高手指点一下更好啦。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"679f44462dccaf4a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250802071522","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录02-缓慢的进度","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":152,"body_sha256":"74b5039aa59ad965b86abfb765604b9cf31cf3e186bbfb55e2c4a2fcbc1ce249"}
-->
今天一看时间，都是八月份了，上次我记录游戏开发的时候还是7月末，简单的说下游戏开发的进度和一些细节吧。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ed38700139d9523a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250803005406","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录03-关于游戏的场景绘制风格的选择","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":371,"body_sha256":"d752bcecc8fe7cb61957ef0172668672c2dc183191f767b423a125d0c155728b"}
-->
只要有空余的时间我就在思考游戏的场景的绘制风格的选择，最开始的时候选择了像素风格，后来换成3D风格采用blender建模，但是感觉3d风格在颜色、材质和动画方面的控制碍于技术原因十分有限，而且风格极难把握，最终还是放弃了，选择了2D风格，但是具体的画风还需要筛选。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"47a879aa6d9a7605","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250817080520","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录04-终于完成了主场景的绘制","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":286,"body_sha256":"e22ef4983db8af4545361becf6b12780d9c7849f19d13a5a626e6c988724b7fb"}
-->
看了一下时间，上次还在纠结场景绘制风格的时间，这一转眼都过去了半个月了，好在，我的游戏主场景基本确定了，人物角色的设计也基本确定了，下图就是游戏的第一次预览图，先复盘一下最近的杂乱的思绪把。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"66936579c8c2514f","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250826152522","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录05-游戏的主线任务代码基本完成","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":301,"body_sha256":"f60f5d530d6183e2647ee6208e3ba3da68db1b55f278fb00f0f8ea2deac402cd"}
-->
经过这个暑假的忙碌，终于基本上完成了游戏的主线代码，也就是模拟博客博主的自身属性的提升，以及发布博文后一些访问量数据的变化，同事模拟了广告联盟的收入，另外测试性的添加了二个随机事件，总之一切还算顺利。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"5904a3930b8f55f8","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250827145453","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录06-游戏中的博客联盟","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":242,"body_sha256":"dfe9c16da86b584e205cc4faef49699c589b2d989265c01e9472fd23585cc032"}
-->
着手开始构建游戏中的博客联盟功能，这让我想起很多当下的博客联盟，目前我比较关注的博客联盟有：十年之约，BlogFinder，博友圈以及记忆中比较认可的就是已经关门大吉的积薪。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a734095483cd5ba0","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250910000235","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录07-复盘与展望","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":255,"body_sha256":"8c1ed68d956b12ef43c15d11962e34847fa6be94022b03da39b64c343d127beb"}
-->
断断续续的写了10天的代码，终于完成了游戏中博客联盟的模拟，博客联盟的编写暂时告一段落，愿望是美好的，现实是残酷的，复盘一下最近的游戏开发进度，规划一下未来的游戏开发方向。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"bb7c39e4aec70721","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250913005315","title":"发小——久违小聚","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":196,"body_sha256":"ebd115f0d1fbd62e0431f006e3101e55a5859bba44eab98d149954c86be2d5b9"}
-->
我有几个非常不错的发小，我们都还生活在这个八县的小城里，每隔一段时间我们就会小聚一下，分享一下自己的生活，也会吐槽一下最近的苦衷。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"15b7d2808c3147c2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250920003603","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录08-第一个有意愿想玩这款游戏的玩家","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":423,"body_sha256":"c95c15db8a52ac66b7c4ba3b8fcf8ca19bdcd6cb383edec8c567a9be8b84e6e4"}
-->
几天前，突然收到了一封来自博客文章评论的邮件提示，内容是：「这个有些期待啊，什么时候好啊？到时候我也想玩一下」，来自网友xpp9的评论。 这是开发这款游戏以来第一位有意愿想玩它的玩家。因为虽然我在博客里记录了游戏制作的过程，但是游戏的具体内容我并没有发布，能有人期待也算是超出了我的意料。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"fb30605c3d762504","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20250923142034","title":"记一次惊险的磁盘操作","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":275,"body_sha256":"69792191fe5eee2664cded7e67973ae1c7b2a98f315fba366f1ebc67401d7319"}
-->
今天安装一个微软的组件库，必须安装在C盘，结果提示我100G的C盘可用容量不足。看了看我可怜的C盘，就只有几百MB的可用容量，清理C盘的软件不是收费就是不好用，只好铤而走险的试试一些奇特的方法了！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
