这几天终于把游戏内主要的几个场景UI以及功能的代码都写完了，目前来说，游戏可以运行直到结束，除了没有剧情和任务外，一个博客的日常操作基本模拟的差不多了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"25bfc6c7ccafe3cd","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251010075131","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录10-关于游戏的主线任务的思考：独立博客的终极意义","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":163,"body_sha256":"b6be7a484d17d175ecf0411b22021ad155056ab2d7f39a9330e3025e441211dd"}
-->
随着代码编写的推进，终于进入到了任务和事件的编写，感觉绕了一大圈又回到了起点。我想起了我创建这个游戏的初衷。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ad86b5ecf1ec7779","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251017235222","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录11-里程碑与成就任务的持续开发","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":166,"body_sha256":"7c9cc439370b0af63647fb57e731afdaa987c596ec704d18a55e67ded3c3aabc"}
-->
这几天搭建好了里程碑成就系统，然后就开始着手编写相应的成就任务了，粗略的算了下，大约会有百十个左右的成就任务。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"78665f5e235561ad","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251030003841","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录12-游戏剧本怎么写？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":265,"body_sha256":"b7b2b89fc937cf7e76716bfc9bb264c104e9a2cd510be58bc239aa831bf3755c"}
-->
上个星期，重写了游戏中的技能升级的方法，改用自己编写的任务系统来触发技能升级，优化了代码和任务的触发，虽然花了些时间，但也是很值得的，但是同时也陷入了整个游戏的剧本编写的深坑。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2ee6fa88978d4882","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251107014030","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录13-完成了游戏的启动与过场","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":280,"body_sha256":"315f2f933da584b4662aae1b31d8e946ba3700cadbc48c8801adcd1ad1da13b9"}
-->
关于游戏的剧本编写，在看过了《游戏剧本怎么写》这本书之后有了一定的启发，虽然之前没接触过剧本的编写，但是一些主线任务的编写也算是有了一些剧情的大概，当务之急是游戏整体进度的推进和完成度。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"53a311630c6455d2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251114010459","title":"在 Godot 4.4 中构建灵活的有限状态机 (FSM)","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":628,"body_sha256":"b98682bed69fd2513502fb2216bc1103513b525ba64faddda3259fd31ed514dc"}
-->
在游戏开发中，角色的行为（如站立、行走、跳跃、攻击）通常需要复杂的逻辑来控制。如果不使用状态机，代码会充斥着大量的 if/else 或 match 语句，导致代码难以阅读、维护和扩展，这就是所谓的“意大利面条式代码”。有限状态机（FSM）提供了一种优雅的解决方案。它将角色的每一个行为定义为一个状态（State），并清晰地定义了状态之间的转换（Transition）规则。本教程将基于 godotfsmtest 项目的代码结构，向您展示如何在 Godot 4.4 中实现一个结构清晰、基于类的 FSM。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"7be34983313df541","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251123135354","title":"无聊的家长会与「资本家」班主任","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":256,"body_sha256":"65f4fba2cf0368b49aba41e278b786303b8f09e96b99b6eb02801963e18dc649"}
-->
孩子上八年级了，这周末期中考试后学校组织开了一次家长会，我觉得挺无聊的而且挺压抑，觉得这个家长会对家长和孩子的帮助并不是很大，反而让人有些反感，大体就是憋得慌那种感觉吧。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

最近几个月，我一直在开发我的第一款独立游戏《我的博客》，在游戏制作中也算是积累了一些经验与心得，所以，我打算制作一个Godot的中文版教程。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

前天，和老丈人一起吃饭的时候聊到马上快冬至了，可以包饺子了，老爷子突然来了一句，冬至不吃饺子，我就问他这是从哪得到的信息？他说抖音上看到的。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

2025年最后一个周六，我起来的比平时要晚很多，八点多我驱车来到离家不到二里地的早市觅食，嗯？这么近为啥要开车？因为太冷了！体感温度大约-25°左右，东北的三九天是真冷啊！用我们这的方言说：这天儿，嘎嘎地冷🥶。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

-->
这几天闲着没事翻别人的博客，看到有个博主说，他这篇文章是躺在床上，用豆包输入法“说”出来的。我一看这操作挺新鲜，立马也在手机上下载了一个试试水。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ed9e010525889d3e","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20251230122510","title":"Godot 4 信号（Signal）的使用入门","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":230,"body_sha256":"0cc57c3095671a6d6fab35cf3c5b72ac81c36bf8b01de8cbd6a8613c3095c14f"}
-->
信号（Signal）是Godot引擎中最强大、最重要的机制之一，它实现了观察者模式，让节点之间可以松耦合地通信。本教程将通过实际案例，带你全面掌握Godot中的信号使用方法。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

今天可真冷啊，不愧是东北的三九天。我干脆窝在家里看书，哪儿也不想去。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"73f7897d8be04fab","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260113010331","title":"脑机接口的另类用途","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":133,"body_sha256":"7f6760dd31c74479d0ab2c082f2d5c6d0f7c25686352fa5f862d5adeab74f682"}

三九天是真的冷，尤其是早上——人已经醒了，身体却不想动，就瘫在床上，脑子却开始胡思乱想。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

今天闲来无事，花了点时间升级一下老程序老代码，让这些老家伙换发一下青春。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

有时候总觉得，要做出一点改变特别难。但其实，只要真正下定决心，事情并没有想象中那么可怕。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6195961ae3ac63ec","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260116011428","title":"Dead Simple Sites 极简网站的搜集与发现","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":119,"body_sha256":"ce5470efb943fe2711e2af8aa4a5d951a58843aa87f38554284f133cfdc0e813"}

Dead Simple Sites,DSS 是我浏览网页是偶然间发现的，它是一个极简网站的搜集与发现的站点。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"fb1be7a0081ce8b4","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260117013926","title":"与孩子一起看《阿甘正传》","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":205,"body_sha256":"4eb2624a90e428499b4b497536b6108d861802756706337748097be14ee477d1"}
-->
东北的三九天，出门玩不太现实。总不能让孩子整天刷手机、打游戏吧？于是想着，不如一起看点经典电影。脑子里第一个蹦出来的，就是《阿甘正传》。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"51d62cf2983ae1c4","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260117131006","title":"这个开源动画图标库，让界面瞬间动起来！","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":151,"body_sha256":"18313ef8675f2361e9a12c306a2ba89ffdbdd40fb59eeb85a6dc6fdf60da97b1"}
-->
最近偶然发现了一个宝藏网站，用了一段时间后，真心觉得它值得每一位开发者、设计师甚至产品经理都试试看！
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"af154ee7c64d2e1c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260118111831","title":"Godot 4 补间动画 (Tween) 指北","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":124,"body_sha256":"f849393263464a7e6796c3805cf31408e10cc62408a600f839887f327c36cecd"}
-->
Godot 4 带来的全新 Tween 系统，摒弃了传统的节点式操作，改用全代码驱动，更加简洁且强大。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1140c514715a7b37","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260119104503","title":"戒烟半个多月，我用AI写代码、用语音写博客","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":241,"body_sha256":"f21a423d64843fb6127c1e9380ee25d90e211edc49d1757598ce278603a4aed4"}
-->
昨天晚上做了个特别真实的梦：梦见遇到一位老朋友，也是个老烟枪。他递给我一根烟，我接过来，抽了一口——真爽啊！聊了几句家常，临走时突然一激灵：“哎呀，我戒烟了！”
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"62e659ea03467b9a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260120060836","title":"如何做一个有逼格的博客？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":196,"body_sha256":"c08160fd15c9cd9fd3367088f21d2f14323f35a68ee49a9dd62e987b425f1358"}
-->
一定得选最冷门的开源框架，雇海外独立设计师，搭就得搭最高调性的静态博客。域名得是 .io 后缀的，服务器直接搁硅谷，存储空间最小也得200G。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"2b566717b250a0d7","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260120142316","title":"使用AI重构我8年前的一个项目:PyDirectoryComparison","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":179,"body_sha256":"e5a94565919262848109b624218d2d583f785bb8f76877453308de79e4739ccc"}
-->
一边玩原神一边就把程序重构了，这个程序是我8年前学习python的时候构建的，当时整整写了一个多星期，BUG很多，界面也是无从下手。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"66674eccd4249bc2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260121015905","title":"博客的主题是设计给谁看的？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":265,"body_sha256":"9a8efa8005ab085ec918230699d0942f5d058d6f3f3782db613e07a9f820dd74"}
-->
差不多每年，我都会忍不住重新折腾一次博客主题。不是因为功能不够用，而是看久了，总觉得哪里不对劲——颜色太刺眼？排版太拥挤？字体不够舒服？于是又冒出一股冲动：要不，做个新主题吧？
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"0a67f889e36b4cb2","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260122010424","title":"手搓代码的时代真的结束了吗？","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":128,"body_sha256":"78f1c4d8ce785b0c7fe78a8757ee03dac7a299577211d9ecdfdc03d7cbbadad1"}
-->
2026 年 1 月 20 日，Node.js 之父 Ryan Dahl 在 X 上发了一条消息：“人类手写代码的时代已经结束。”
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"4702587537a1fb39","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260122111009","title":"Godot游戏开发《我的博客》记录15 使用AI分析游戏后续开发并给出建议","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":326,"body_sha256":"71b5bc5fa30c6b07c45262b67a7e75cf03ee078a2f407914c7a7f649130229b4"}
-->
去年一直心心满满、斗志昂扬要开发的「游戏大作」《我的博客》，终于被疲惫的开发倦意打败了。但是我没有放弃，所以，我决定后续的开发将大量借助甚至全部由 AI 来编写游戏的代码。或许这就是我最终的解救，我只是希望把这个游戏做完。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"eb389a7dfcfb850c","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260123012927","title":"那些幼稚的过往，都是成长的温柔印记","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":238,"body_sha256":"7b4a9cf600a68b98e49edb291670bc15a58449fbd1e4defcb1a0f0009bba767b"}
-->
前几天关注了一个博客，那位博主竟然把自己的所有数据都给炸了，以至于我还没有来得及翻看以前的内容，真是可惜了。不过昨天浏览发现博客有重新开放了，也算是一件好事。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"655507f7fe307b18","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260125074853","title":"关于AI辅助Godot游戏制作的一些看法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":240,"body_sha256":"c31ff72643cf4a0abdf5701d9892501d7a22f0379bf0b7787363c107c1fa3143"}
-->
AI辅助编程已经不是什么新鲜事了，去年开始很多程序员甚至是普通人都开始了**Vibe Coding**,对于普通的简单的项目来说，AI接管会很轻松，但是复杂项目并非简单，也有可能出乱子。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"be804082391d2fb8","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260126133512","title":"博客的极致折腾","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":160,"body_sha256":"c8bdfee976cbac617231da1f652c7eb290910cd9181337aef6e2a79addc9d7f4"}
-->
断断续续地折腾博客有一个星期了，似乎每年都会有段时间，会集中精力对博客现有的一些细节不断修改、翻新、打磨。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"6eed7f0042597028","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260129012840","title":"不折腾了，设计了一个极简主题","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":299,"body_sha256":"5bf660cc070c5f6f003b36ff3bc913c1391db9bec02756a6e7d5dc80c59112f4"}
-->
主题改的心烦，因为一直在添加新的效果，总觉的不够cool，或是与原有的设计不搭配，这该死的完美主义与半吊子设计和技术的死缠烂打本来就是个错误，所以，我推翻了之前的所有设计方案，从头再来，先做减法在做设计。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"e511069896bf7bd1","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260130014503","title":"网页加载的时候获取giscus当前页面的评论数","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":169,"body_sha256":"728c617ede21e37d7345528898a8c88440082e51cc0b99c08d494906948bb010"}
-->
前几天看到有个博客使用giscus，而且他的文章页打开后可以获得giscus的评论数，很是感兴趣，也就照猫画虎的实现了这个功能。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"ac6ad2c0302b9d9a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260130103859","title":"基于 Next.js + Supabase 的文章阅读量和喜欢功能","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":162,"body_sha256":"542e4f9c51f7a454307bd0d89fe7ab2ae63958afafc156bad298f2eb28ccb21d"}
-->
详细介绍如何在实际项目中实现文章阅读量和喜欢功能，包括Supabase数据库创建、API开发、前端组件实现以及Vercel部署配置
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"61eaabc883c8200b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260131102452","title":"一月小计，活着","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":230,"body_sha256":"47aa76b8c73f14c34622e5ddb5e3b62ed9c1823177efbfa14121b2bfc558fb1c"}
-->
博文的一转眼到三十一号了，马上就要到二月份。仔细想了想，从开年到现在，好像做了很多，但又没做什么。好像挺忙，但又都是在瞎忙。那么，我1月份都做了什么？简介
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"d6c76682c7ee3e43","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260201082442","title":"注销了四个平台的「花呗」","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":124,"body_sha256":"85a528e4041fbcb3ecdd87cff433c94b88e23a55c9432162a7eac82055e9f971"}
-->
就在刚刚，我注销了拼多多的先用后付、美团的美团月付、支付宝的花呗，还有京东的白条。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"1afcff145b69c6a8","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260202003538","title":"PageSpeed Insights 满分攻略","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":183,"body_sha256":"c9eda64b29291556db559a9b4c1856847887de77c4bfc17fe42bb04706befa79"}
-->
这次设计的主题还有一个小小的意外，那就是Google PageSpeed Insights（PSI）竟然拿到了4个100分的满分，有心栽花花不开，无心插柳柳成荫啊。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"b6379af7c9b86103","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260203070348","title":"给你的文章添加 AI 创作等级标识","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":134,"body_sha256":"80f4b9ce7c8ea01c6dbf61ec6f28cfd406591f4d37ac124e26f7662b475ca65d"}
-->
独立博客以真实和独立为根本，有必要告知访客正在读的内容是不是 AI 生成，AI 都参与了哪些部分。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"52d98252c6ac4e4b","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260204112608","title":"@tailwindcss/typography 提供美观的默认排版样式库","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":281,"body_sha256":"f83121b841051eeedb84b2ed8d4c923c56bd69bbe82516824a40f9a95c5a4119"}
-->
之前，一直对文章页的markdown排版样式不满意（自己编写的css样式），总觉得不够专业进而影响阅读体验，直到发现了@tailwindcss/typography，这玩意简直就是行业的标杆。然后就直接使用了，珍惜生命，拒绝造轮子。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

我一直认为，能否极简地生活，要看你能否辨别出什么才是真正重要的东西。能够分辨什么是重要之物，就是一种智慧，就是明禅理。—《禅与极简生活艺术》

本来打算用 Astro 重构博客，但发现 Next.js 也能实现同样的静态化效果。本文分享了选择 Next.js 静态化的理由和完整的改造过程。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"a12a3b307308fedd","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260208031913","title":"一分钟美化 RSS：使用 RSS.Beauty 让你的博客 RSS 源更漂亮","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":196,"body_sha256":"eae2487a99a260f618b7948816a329129b31d143e135f6d568aac8ae2a8bad85"}
-->
RSS.Beauty 是一个在线服务，可以为 RSS 源自动生成漂亮的 XSLT 样式表。本文介绍如何使用 RSS.Beauty 快速为博客 RSS 添加美化效果，无需编写任何代码。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

简单来说，Mastodon（长毛象） 是一个开源的、去中心化的分布式微博客平台。如果你注册登录，第一感觉他就像Twitter/X或者微博一样。

昨天闲来无事，翻看微信朋友圈，突然间看到这样一个朋友圈，上面配了两只小狗，底下配着文字「狗逼家长，当面一套背后一套！」。

这篇指南将带你从零开始，在 Ubuntu 系统上搭建一套完全私有化、具备高颜值交互界面的 AI 对话系统。我们将使用 **Ollama** 作为后端引擎，部署阿里最新的 **Qwen 3.5 (9B)** 模型，并配合 **Open WebUI** 实现类 ChatGPT 的操作体验。

<!-- /rss-aggr -->

-->
最近，养虾确实活了，也出现了一些负面的新闻。其实大家大可不必过于焦虑，养个虾米而已，不至于老鼠过街人人喊打吧？
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"b67a81d685153c79","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260315104644","title":"OpenClaw劝退指南：这10类人，请放过工具也放过自己","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":398,"body_sha256":"e7bb92c5c8938e83f3e86de50d55e8964c6fe66858e7ce2201130932dd90ef6c"}
-->
最近，OpenClaw火了，火得一塌糊涂。不过这个火爆也引发了诸多的安全问题，最近半个月，国家层面至少有三个权威机构/平台就OpenClaw发出风险提示或预警。但是OpenClaw真的有那么危险可怕吗？抛开这些风险我们暂时不说，依然有10类人不适合安装部署使用OpenClaw。这10类人，请放过工具也放过自己吧。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"b9db4267f83e0136","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260316065533","title":"养龙虾（OpenClaw使用）感受","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":234,"body_sha256":"ce1f872095bad677f89236ce66a7c12ea1ed3b18324d14aa596cb239fc44b8a0"}
-->
断断续续部署使用OpenClaw也有段日子了。刚部署完那会儿正值腾讯排队安装，紧接着国家发布了风险提示和预警，但这些丝毫没挡住养一只龙虾的欲望。说说这周养虾的感受吧。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"9bb64cbf422402e3","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260316113823","title":"Lucy（我的OpenClaw）的自我进化风险","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":140,"body_sha256":"eab112962ac9b101149308c38854694b64360cf6089c4a3fea2c80ef22c859c1"}
-->
前两天发现了一件挺有意思的事，我的龙虾去 InStreet 访问的时候，竟然被论坛上别的龙虾的帖子影响了。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"795db937a7e5e54a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260317074500","title":"当博客失去人类的灵魂，请给博客一个诚实的标签","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":186,"body_sha256":"726870874df22ccc4845acfef7a4fbb5976e04b2236e52878c05eae2b9bbe304"}
-->
我个人的建议：保护个人独立博客这片人类的净土，不建议使用AI完全控制生成维护博客和博文。这样，个人独立博客就失去了存在的意义。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"32b354313ed9f333","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260320161757","title":"「十日后」Vol.2 · 春天","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":200,"body_sha256":"02f8bcbe4576706425aaa62a8bd05c5d57e0d80dc100e7ffc7a845a89e50a1ab"}

十天，足够一些事情发生与改变。本期推荐 XHunt AI 趋势平台、SkillHub、InStreet 三个工具，分享《断舍离》《沉思录》读书心得，以及 OpenClaw 使用体验。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"5762c0e2c0b13f4a","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260320190747","title":"OpenClaw 必装 10 大技能：让你的龙虾从能用变好用","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":189,"body_sha256":"767973d528c90f8c37ea82be989cff115ce1378c44023fe17c2374841dfe5782"}
-->
装好 OpenClaw 只是第一步，真正让它干活的是 Skills。这 10 个技能是必装的：搜索类、内容处理类、效率工具类，让你的 Agent 从"能用"变"好用"。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"8b8929d88fe1b096","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/20260321104756","title":"马可·奥勒留和他的《沉思录》","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":274,"body_sha256":"ca5b637d63f7e9211159678690df5066e3c864515e7d0ddc3f3a6de6bda4b410"}
-->
如果你要读《沉思录》，就必须了解这本书的作者和作者所处的背景历史。《沉思录》（Meditations，希腊文原题 Τὰ εἰς ἑαυτόν，意为“写给自己的话”），也就是说，这是一本哲学家皇帝的自言自语。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

iflow凉了，油价涨了。

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->

{"v":1,"guid":"63b93af20a6c19d1","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/32","title":"Python多线程编程（上）多线程创建的几种方法","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":232,"body_sha256":"cb9b39e89fd502fc691ae96f7368dc3b6baa2d1f675ddfce1305fda21fa67db1"}
-->
在编程的日常中，如果遇到大量相同并且重复的计算任务时，我们考虑使用多线程，多线程可以并发的执行程序中的函数，这样就可以更快的利用CPU计算结果，结省时间成本。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"30d305827b2f1040","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/33","title":"Python多线程编程（下）多线程锁 threading.Lock","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":289,"body_sha256":"9cf132f778cdd41fac4f32efbe6cf3cf822867c421cecb2983eff29a8e5caded"}
-->
在多线程任务中，线程对数据的操作是随机的，这个先后次序无法预测，如果利用多线程修改唯一数据，由于对数据操作的随机性，必会影响到数据结果的准确性，所以在多线程的任务的编码中，我们必须使用线程锁。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
学习套接字编程最好的方法就是从实际应用的开发中边学边用，这样对TCP服务器及客户端通信中遇到的知识点才会更加了解。

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

协程定义说的清楚明了的文章不是很多，手头上有几本Python相关的书籍，其中流畅的Python一书中解释协程的定义是我认为最简单明了的。

<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
这阵子一直在看有关Python相关的书，Python上手确实很简单，但这并不意味着Python是一门浅显的编程语言，语法简练确实让我们节省了大量的时间。不过当你深入的学习Python的时候，比如：装饰器，推导，多线程及多进程，协程，高阶函数，元编程等等等等很多，你会发现，Python的深入学习并不是很简单。会写代Python和会优雅的编写Python代码是两回事，出来混的，坑迟早还是要挖的，这不前几天协程的深入学习，搞的我很不爽，到现在还是一头雾水。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
pygame的框架使用起来很简单，但这个框架功能也还算可以的，虽然网上对pygame评价褒贬不一，但对于其的存在必有其的存在意义，功能上虽然不能说是十分的完美，但对于新手来说，在使用和学习中还是很有价值的。

pygame写起游戏都是函数式编写，对于一些简单的小游戏或许可以应付，随着游戏内容的增加，我们不可能只在一个.py文件中写下所有的游戏代码，这个时候，我们应该考虑对游戏中组件及对象进行封装，用面向对象的方式来进行游戏代码的编写。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"124a14dbcc163154","feed_id":"suiyan.cc-067f0c16fa98","feed_url":"https://suiyan.cc/rss.xml","site_url":"https://suiyan.cc/","feed_title":"碎言博客","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://www.suiyan.cc/blog/71","title":"“编学编玩”用Pygame编写游戏（7）Pingball弹球小游戏","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":399,"body_sha256":"99e2e657b60875ebaa5c084ea0e164547552926c2ee502cf8d84db1af4d7d49e"}
-->
以前学java的时候用java做了一个简单弹球小游戏，觉得还是蛮有意思的，这次用pygame重写一下试试，以前觉得游戏编程很简单，可能是考虑的太简单了，游戏的编写是步步为营，逻辑紧扣，错一点游戏都无法运行的，所以学习用面向对象方式编写游戏，是对python编程学习的强化练习，大家可以多试试。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->

# 血液循环

* * *

## 一、心动周期

![心动周期](https://fallen.wang/p/cardiac-cycle.png)

### 判断心动周期阶段

阶段划分：
    
    
    1
    2
    3
    4
    5
    6
    

| 
    
    
    1993N3A心动周期中从房室瓣关闭到开放的时间相当于：E
    A.心房收缩期+心室等容收缩期
    B.心房收缩期
    C.心室收缩期
    D.心室舒张期
    E.心室收缩期+等容舒张期
      
  
---|---  
  
各期特点，如动脉压、室内压、心室容积、瓣膜开放或关闭时间、心音的形成：
    
    
    1
    2
    3
    4
    5
    6
    

| 
    
    
    1994N15A心动周期中在下列哪个时期主动脉压最低：A
    A.等容收缩期末
    B.等容舒张期末
    C.心房收缩期末
    D.快速充盈期末
    E.减慢充盈期末
      
  
---|---  
特征 | 时期  
---|---  
左心室压力MAX | 快速射血期末  
左心室容积MIN | 等容舒张期末  
左心室容积MAX | 心房收缩期末  
主动脉压MAX | 快速射血期末  
主动脉压MIN | 等容收缩期末  
主动脉血流量MAX | 快速射血期  
室内压升高最快 | 等容收缩期  
第一心音产生 | 心房收缩期末（房室瓣关闭）  
第二心音产生 | 减慢射血期末（主动脉瓣关闭）  
  
### 评价心功能的指标

射血分数、心输出量、每搏输出量、心指数、心脏做功量：
    
    
    1
    2
    3
    4
    5
    6
    

| 
    
    
    2005N6A高血压患者与正常人相比哪项指标明显升高：E
    A.每搏输出量
    B.射血分数
    C.心输出量
    D.心指数
    E.心脏做功量
      
  
---|---  
  
搏出量即每搏输出量，约60~80ml。影响因素有前负荷、后负荷、心肌收缩能力和回心血量等。

射血分数是搏出量除以心室舒张末期容积。扩心病患者心室舒张末期容积变大，射血分数降低。因此射血分数对早期心脏泵血功能异常具有重要意义。

**单侧** 心室每分钟输出的血液量称为心输出量。数值等于心率乘以搏出量。左右两侧心输出量基本相等。

以单位面积计算的心输出量为心指数。静息心指数用于评价不同个体的心功能。

# 最近在看

[ ![](https://windypath.com/images/deep_thinking.jpg) 想想就开心！ ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/deep_thinking) [ ![](https://windypath.com/images/honor_yourself.jpg) 尊重自己：给予与接收的心灵艺术 ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/honor_yourself) [ ![](https://windypath.com/images/the_one_thing.jpg) 最重要的事，只有一件 ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/the_one_thing)

# 最喜欢的书籍

[ ![](https://windypath.com/images/book_csapp.jpg) 深入理解计算机系统 ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/book_csapp) [ ![](https://windypath.com/images/deep_thinking.jpg) 想想就开心！ ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/deep_thinking) [ ![](https://windypath.com/images/book_worth_living.jpg) 人间值得 ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/book_worth_living) [ ![](https://windypath.com/images/book_courage_to_be_hated.jpg) 被讨厌的勇气 ](https://windypath.com/bookdigest/book_courage_to_be_hated)
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"44ba78ce7a59a5f1","feed_id":"windypath.com-23465ecdf39b","feed_url":"https://windypath.com/rss.xml","site_url":"https://windypath.com","feed_title":"风萧古道","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://windypath.com/about/","title":"关于","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":2367,"body_sha256":"e2728a59663b34962876ced61b053369ce4e5f4008648355bf784b7c84e4228c"}
-->
95后，男，福建厦门人，FJNU毕业，现居上海。

7岁时家里第一次买了电脑。当双手放在鼠标和键盘上，我隐约意识到这东西与其他家电不同。“计算机”这个名字，比只能算数的“计算器”有趣得多。虽然没有游戏天赋，连《魔兽争霸3》都玩不明白，但上网和信息课却让我着迷。四年级学会保存 Word 文档后，我第一次保存了网页的 html 文件，用记事本修改代码，再次打开时发现页面真的变了。那一刻的惊喜，至今难忘。

我并不聪明，也没什么编程天赋。十四岁时在书店翻看网页制作的技术书，靠死记硬背理解 HTML 和 CSS。2012 年想做自己的网站，却受限于年龄、金钱和能力，只能在免费空间里折腾半成品，为 JavaScript 的各种特效感到神奇。高中时，同学已经能用 VB 写小游戏，我却连最简单的逻辑都想不出来。

高考后，我几乎毫不犹豫地选择了计算机相关专业。但大学后两年迷茫又浮躁，把 git 当 svn 用，把 GitHub 当素材库，抄代码却不求甚解。完美主义和自尊心让我不愿承认“我不会”。毕业后在厦门工作了一年半，本以为生活会就此平稳，却始终放不下对计算机的热爱和对“成为高手”的渴望。

后来辗转多地，从独立维护服务器、深夜热更新修 Bug，到转向 Go，又回到 Java，在不同团队和环境中反复碰壁、挣扎与成长。压力、自我怀疑和错误选择贯穿其中，但也正是在不断下沉中打磨技术，等待机会。

或许性格、心态和家庭影响让我走了不少弯路，但从 7 岁握住鼠标开始，我对游戏、软件和计算机的热爱从未减退。“30 岁前成为高级软件工程师”，是我大学刚开学时当众说出的目标，我不会辜负它。

学软件十年，写博客十年，却没有真正独立维护的开源项目，GitHub 上更多是 fork 和未完成的尝试。这就是一个普通软件人的真实写照。

勤学苦练，年复一年，从未是错。过去没做到的，现在会做到，以后也会做到。我将在这里，记录自己一步步成长的过程。

# 工作技能

编程语言: Golang, Java, Kotlin, Python, Shell, SQL

技术栈: Akka, MongoDB, MySQL, Redis, SpringBoot

英语：大学英语六级（CET-6）

# 工作经历

## 字节投资的2年创业公司

类征途MMO Golang服务器开发工程师 上海 类征途MMO是一款多人国战MMORPG网络游戏，目前已在微信小程序测试中。 2023年9月 – 2023年11月

主要负责工作：

  1. 使用 Golang 开发敏感词检测模块，读取100,000条敏感词，构建敏感词检测 Trie 字典树并将其缓存到二进制文件，实现毫秒内完成敏感词匹配，与 Github 其他同类模块（importcjj/sensitive，antlinker/go-dirtyfilter ）相比匹配速度快25%；
  2. 快速上手 Mqant 微服务框架，熟悉进程间通信机制，配合使用 MongoDB和 Redis ，开发 MMORPG 战场玩法，实现100名玩家同屏争夺首领尾刀奖励的功能；
  3. 配合策划开发新功能，管理员玩家能够将地图内 NPC 外观替换为指定玩家外观。同时快速熟悉玩家的养成维度，整理任务类型，和策划配合完善玩家养成模块的开发，包括新手任务、主线任务、礼包、活动等。

## 某上市游戏公司

SLG手游 Java服务器开发执行主程序 上海 SLG手游是一款战争策略SLG网络游戏，目前已在Google Play上架3年。 2020年12月 – 2023年5月

主要负责工作：

  1. 深入参与 SLG 游戏的沙盘玩法开发，包含大地图 AOI 算法和状态同步功能，优化行军及行军线、怪物刷新循环、AI 行为等大功能模块；
  2. 精通 Java 。就职期间基于 Akka Cluster 微服务框架开发服务器，使用 MySQL和 Redis 存储服务器数据。主导了一个大型跨服副本玩法的实现（百名玩家同地图抢怪），完成其中的玩家匹配功能和 Boss 特殊争夺机制（驻扎积攒势力值，满足条件后才可攻击）；
  3. 与策划协作进行战斗模块的开发和优化，实现多种新技能机制，简化战斗协议数据结构，实现战报大小缩减10%。协助策划打印线上战报信息，排查战斗 Bug 。完善了内城养成模块（英雄、装备、科技、任务等）及外城沙盘玩法，开发了老玩家回归活动；
  4. 为了提升开发效率，增加测试时间，重构了项目已有的活动框架代码，去除冗余结构。在不影响线上游戏运营的情况下，节约了开发时间，重构后，活动开发时间缩短一半；
  5. 拥有丰富的线上问题排查经验，善于从玩家反馈、后台日志、代码和配置表中找到问题所在。配合运维跟进版本的更新，监控服务器环境和日志，随时解决问题；
  6. 工作态度认真负责，及时排查和修复线上 Bug，7*24小时监听线上客服反馈。于2022年9月起独立负责服务器开发，且负责期间无线上重大 Bug 发生。

## 某创业板上市公司

云API接口开放平台 Vue + SpringBoot全栈开发 福建厦门 整合集团各个子公司提供的API接口到一个统一的平台中进行管理和使用。 2019年5月 – 2020年11月

负责工作：

  1. 参与开发外部接口的安全传输功能。使用 OAuth 2.0协议标准生成 access_token ，用 Redis 暂存 access_token 并设置过期时间，使用 RSA与 AES 加密算法保证安全传输；
  2. 使用 Vue + SpringBoot 开发 API 接口的管理系统，并将用户数据信息及接口使用情况统计存储在 MySQL中；
  3. 负责版本更新，使用 Nginx 反向代理将其部署到服务器。

# 教育经历

# 豆瓣链接

<https://read.douban.com/ebook/132946207/>

# 读后感

这是一本教人如何尊重自己的书。

我们从小到大接收的教育都是尊重别人，而很少有人告诉我们，要尊重自己。

尊重自己？这听起来是有点奇怪的。自己又不是别人，为何要“尊重”？而事实上，由于我们自己过于了解自己，所以不自觉经常委屈了自己。

这本书里会讨论每一个人内心中思考过的一些问题，然后提出“谬论”和“真谛”。再对其进行分析探讨，让你对自己的内心有一个全新的认知。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1

-->
> 最近入手了树莓派 4B，打算基于树莓派做一些有趣&有用的项目，该系列笔记来记录个人树莓派学习和开发的历程。

连载确认！该系列文章将会从装机开始，介绍树莓派用于以下几个项目的开发和部署过程：

  * 基于 Docker 的家庭服务器
  * 智能家居控制中枢
  * 影音中心 & 电视机顶盒
  * 物联网 or AI 的衍生应用实践

本篇首先介绍亮机和开发环境设置过程，包含：组装、系统安装、远程开发环境设置（SSH、XRDP）

## 树莓派组装

树莓派组装上比较防呆接口，唯一需要注意的是散热块和风扇的安装：

  * 在 CPU 和缓存等模块上装上散热片
  * 风扇的固定位置在外壳上，接线如下图所示
  * SD 卡的安装位置在背面

[ ![微信图片_20230626091027.jpg](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/%E5%BE%AE%E4%BF%A1%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87_20230626091027.jpg) ](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/%E5%BE%AE%E4%BF%A1%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87_20230626091027.jpg)

## 系统安装

> 该部分仅介绍我使用的系统安装和烧录软件，系统的选择需要根据自己所拥有的设备，以及目的去选择。

硬件准备：SD 卡，读卡器，一台正常运行的 PC，可外接的屏幕。

### 系统烧录软件安装

[Raspberry-Software](https://www.raspberrypi.com/software/) : 安装 Raspberry Pi Image 系统烧录软件

[ ![image.png](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625171352.png) ](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625171352.png)

### 系统选择和安装

使用读卡器将 SD 卡连接至电脑，即可通过烧录软件选择系统进行安装：

[ ![image.png](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625172206.png) ](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625172206.png)

耐心等待即可。

## 远程开发环境设置

开发环境主要需要注意的是以下几项设置，实现了如下的设置之后就可以在没有显示器的条件下实现对树莓派的开发和控制。

  * SSH 开放远程连接，实现远程开发环境
  * Proxy 设置，这里使用 Ubuntu 读取局域网代理服务器的方案。
  * Docker 项目部署和实现
  * 【optional】XRDP、VNC 远程桌面连接
  * 【optional】Zerotier 等方案实现内网穿透
  * 【optional】文件同步（rsync）、共享（smb）方案

SSH 的部分参考 [[Published发布/SSH]] 一文中的远程权限开放设置，以及免密登陆配置，方便对树莓派的远程连接，这里就不再赘述。

Proxy 部分可以参考本文的解决方案，基于 ubuntu 系统设置全局的网络代理服务器，减少在终端环境中需要为多个不同 CLI 工具设置代理的压力，需要注意的是，Docker 的代理设置还是需要独立进行配置，参考下面的 docker 一文。

Docker 安装的部分参考 [[Published发布/在Linux中安装Docker]] 一文进行 Linux 环境下的 Docker 和对应的 Dashboard：portainer 的安装和测试。

### XRDP、VNC 设置

这里推荐 XRDP 做 Windows 和 Linux 之间的远程桌面连接，GUI 界面首选，我这里只简单介绍以下 XRDP 远程连接的方式。

参考连接：[如何在Ubuntu 20.04上安装Xrdp服务器 | myfreax](https://www.myfreax.com/how-to-install-xrdp-on-ubuntu-20-04/)

> Xrdp 是 Microsoft 远程桌面协议（RDP）的开源实现

### 安装 Xrdp 以及验证

直接使用 apt 安装xrdp

bash 
    
    
    sudo apt-get install xrdp

安装完成后，服务应该会自动启动，可以输入下列的内容进行验证：

bash 
    
    
    sudo systemctl status xrdp

输出将如下所示，则服务已经运行了：

[ ![image.png](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625174518.png) ](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625174518.png)

如果服务还未运行，尝试将 xrdp 用户添加到 ssl-cert 组，然后重启服务

bash 
    
    
    sudo adduser xrdp ssl-cert
    sudo systemctl restart xrdp

至此已经安装完成了，其默认监听 3389 端口，如果系统上配备了防火墙，则需要打开其监听的端口，同时要允许从指定的 IP 地址和 IP 范围来访问其服务器。可以运行以下的命令

bash 
    
    
    sudo ufw allow from 192.168.33.0/24 to any port 3389

xrdp 服务运行起来以后需要添加对应的用户用来登录：

bash 
    
    
    sudo adduser aiken

这里可能会遇到使用 windows 远程登录之后，输入密码以后**蓝屏没有进入主界面** 的现象，这可能是由于 [Root 用户远程登录的默认问题](https://www.reddit.com/r/raspberry_pi/comments/qw1cdw/raspberry_pi_4_xrdp_windows_10_remote_desktop/) ，有以下两种方式可以解决：

  1. 可以通过使用别的账户进行登录即可。

bash 
    
    
    sudo adduser others

  1. （推荐）修改远程连接的配置，实现主用户可正常登录远程桌面

bash 
    
    
    sudo adduser xrdp ssl-cert
    sudo vim /etc/X11/xrdp/xorg.conf

找到 Option DRMDevice 行，并将其选项修改为如下

conf 
    
    
     Option "DRMDevice" ""

重启树莓派，即可实现远程桌面的正常连接。

### Windows 连接远程桌面

  * Win+S+‘mstsc’| 远程桌面连接 输入指定的 IP 即可打开对应的远程连接页面，输入 Ubuntu 的对应密码即可进入远程桌面。

[ ![image.png](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625175728.png) ](https://picture-bed-001-1310572365.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/3070PC/20230625175728.png)

### 内网穿透方案

使用 zerotier 执行初步的内网穿透方案，可以参考 [[Published发布/NAT穿透]]一文

### 文件同步&共享方案

该部分实际上主要介绍两种方案：RSYNC 文件夹同步 & SMB 文件共享

【不推荐】**基于 RSYNC 和 SSH 免密登陆和计划任务** ，来进行文件夹的同步：这样会在两个系统上存有同个文件的备份，也可以选择同步删除和上传，保持文件夹的一致性。

但是该方案实际上不太符合树莓派的使用场景：除非需要做备份，否则通常仅有少量的文件传输场景，便于环境配置，因此跑计划任务算是比较多余的一件事。

如果有相关需求的话可以参考该文 [[Published发布/Transfer-Sync-Files]]

【推荐】**使用 SMB 进行文件的共享** ：这种方案实际上就是 Windows 的共享文件夹策略，方便局域网内的各个设备进行访问。

windows 共享文件的设置和 Ubuntu 上访问共享文件夹的方式可以参考[[Published发布/局域网文件共享]]进行配置

# November 10, 2025 — 今天看到社会达尔文主义者发了一个让人疑惑的朋友圈

Add your notes here…

陈虎平应该是我意识到的第一个社会达尔文主义者。但是今天看到他的朋友圈：

“伟大不是金钱、名气或权力，而是善行。

善良无价。”

“Greatness does not come about through accumulating great amounts of money, great amounts of publicity or great power in government. When you help someone in any of thousands of ways, you help the world. Kindness is costless but also priceless. ”

—— Warren Buffett

# October 29, 2025 — Luna got croup and has had fever for the past 4 days.

Add your notes here…

It started last Friday night. Luna had a fever, though I didn’t notice at first — it was Hao who felt her body burning against his arm. That evening, she had come home wearing only a single layer of clothing. I felt a pang of guilt when I realized I hadn’t told Ms. Hamideh that there was a change of clothes in Luna’s orange bag. If she had known, she might have helped Luna switch into something warmer instead of keeping her in that thin outfit.

At the beginning, I thought the fever was just from teething. So on Saturday, I didn’t do much besides giving her Tylenol. But by Sunday, my worry grew. I scheduled a virtual appointment with a nurse practitioner, who told me that Luna’s symptoms were likely viral — and that I should give her Motrin instead.

Sunday night was the hardest. Luna cried for more than an hour and a half. At one point, I was ready to take her to the ER. Her breathing had a sharp, whistling sound that scared me. I searched online and even asked ChatGPT, and everything pointed toward going to the hospital. But somehow, Hao managed to calm her down and put her to sleep.

On Monday morning, I called the clinic as soon as it opened and got an appointment with Dr. Min for 12:20 p.m. Her ear temperature was 100.4°F and her oxygen level 96. The doctor said 97–100 is within a normal range, and only below 93 is concerning. He listened to her lungs — thankfully, they were clear — but her throat was swollen. Because of the noisy breathing, he diagnosed her with **croup** , a viral infection.

There wasn’t much medicine for it except **steroids**. I hesitated. The word itself felt heavy, unnatural. But Dr. Min assured me that the dosage was very small — just 5 ml for three days — and safe. So we agreed. Luna’s condition was a little better that day, though her fever lingered and we continued giving her Motrin.

We didn’t actually start the steroid until Tuesday. That day, Luna’s formula intake dropped drastically. It took a while to realize why — Hao had mixed the steroid into her milk, and it made the formula bitter. I should have tasted it earlier, when I saw her try to drink, take just a few sips, and stop again. Once we threw away the medicated formula, she began drinking normally again. She finished her evening bottle and seemed calmer.

But that night, around 9 p.m., her coughing worsened. One violent cough triggered reflux, and she vomited. I rushed to her room and arrived just in time to see her stir in her sleep, about to throw up. I lifted her upright and held her close as she vomited the milk. It broke my heart — but at least she could breathe again.

Today, finally, her fever was gone. She still coughed and had a runny nose, but she was clearly improving. I didn’t give her any medicine today. Her appetite was back, and her formula intake was much better. I took the day off to care for her, but by evening, I felt utterly drained.

Yesterday, there was a big layoff at Amazon. I wasn’t affected, but it left a strange heaviness in me. Maybe next time I won’t be so lucky. The world feels uncertain, and sometimes I wonder — what is the true value of human beings in this new era of machines?

I keep thinking that I want to spend more time on things that feel meaningful — time with my family, time creating, time being human.

Later, I went to the Santa Clara Library at Central Park. The air there felt calm and familiar, filled with the quiet rustle of pages. I caught myself thinking — if I could be a librarian, how peaceful that would be. Surrounded by books, by knowledge, by the rhythm of silence. I felt a small whisper inside me say: _this is the kind of life I want — one rooted in learning, in love, and in stillness._

* * *

# October 25, 2025 — I feel the urgency of writing and recording my life

Add your notes here…

As AI continues to evolve, I feel a growing urgency to write and record my daily life. Even if it’s just a simple log of what I did in a day, it gives me a sense of fulfillment—a reminder that I am living, that I exist.

Today, Luna was quite fussy and ran a slight fever. I hope it’s just due to teething rather than anything else. I noticed her second tooth coming in—the second bottom front tooth. She cried a lot last night and continued this morning. When I measured her temperature with the sensor thermometer, it showed 100.3°F. I gave her Tylenol twice—once around 2 p.m. before her nap, and again before bedtime at around 7 p.m. Hopefully, it’s nothing more than teething.

I also made **zongzi** today—a traditional Chinese sticky rice dumpling filled with savory pork, mushrooms, and scallops, wrapped in bamboo leaves. I steamed them in the pressure cooker for 80 minutes, and they turned out delicious. I used the remaining pork to make **red-braised pork belly**. Since it’s Hao’s birthday, I also cooked him **seafood braised noodles**. I’m proud of myself for managing to prepare all of this while caring for Luna.

Luna was so sweet—after her morning nap, she sat quietly watching me make zongzi. At one point, she had such a serious expression on her face that I wondered what she was thinking… until I realized she was just pooping! It made me laugh out loud.

Another reflection I had today came from the audiobook _Brain Rules for Baby._ It reminded me how important it is for parents to praise their children for **effort** , not for being “smart.” Intelligence is a fixed trait that’s largely out of a child’s control, but effort is something they can choose and develop. Studies also show that being “smart” doesn’t guarantee academic or life success—but consistent effort often does.

What I hope to start doing soon is **strength training**. I already have an Apple Fitness+ subscription, so there’s really no excuse not to start. Whenever I hesitate, I’ll remember Mel Robbins’s _Five-Second Rule_ : “Five, four, three, two, one—go.”

A more literacy and introspective version from AI:

### Reflections

As artificial intelligence advances with astonishing speed, I find myself increasingly aware of the quiet urgency to write—to leave traces of my own human days. The world feels like it’s accelerating toward abstraction, and yet, through the simple act of recording my daily life, I anchor myself in the tangible. Even a humble log of what I’ve done becomes proof that I existed, that I moved through time with purpose, that I was alive.

Today, Luna was fussy and feverish. Her little body felt warm against my arm, her cheeks flushed with heat. I hope it’s just teething—nothing more. I noticed the faint white ridge of her second tooth emerging on her bottom gum. She cried through the night and into the morning, her tiny sobs punctuating the silence of our home. When I checked her temperature, the thermometer glowed 100.3°F. I gave her Tylenol twice—once before her nap, once before bed—and hoped her body would soon find rest.

Between soothing her cries, I wrapped myself in the comfort of cooking. I made **zongzi** , the traditional sticky rice dumplings of my childhood—filled with savory pork, mushrooms, and scallops, and wrapped carefully in bamboo leaves. The scent of steaming rice filled the kitchen, carrying with it memories of festivals and family gatherings far away. I also made **red-braised pork belly** with the leftover meat, and, since it was Hao’s birthday, a bowl of **seafood braised noodles**. Somehow, between Luna’s fussiness and her fleeting smiles, I managed it all. I felt quietly proud—proof that I can still create small beauty amid the chaos of motherhood.

After her nap, Luna sat beside me, watching as I tied each zongzi with thread. Her gaze was so serious, so absorbed, as if she were studying the ceremony of life itself. Then, with perfect comic timing, her solemn expression shifted—and I realized she had simply pooped. I couldn’t help laughing. These moments—tiny, imperfect, yet utterly real—fill my heart in ways I never expected.

Later, while folding laundry, I listened to the audiobook _Brain Rules for Baby._ A line stayed with me: praise children for their **effort** , not their **intelligence**. “Smart” is static—a label beyond their control—but effort is a choice, a habit, a muscle to be strengthened. Intelligence may open doors, but persistence is what carries us through them. I want Luna to grow up knowing that her worth lies not in being perfect, but in trying, failing, and trying again.

As evening settled, I thought about my own efforts—the ones I keep postponing. I’ve subscribed to Apple Fitness+, and yet I keep waiting for the “right time” to begin. Perhaps there is no perfect time. Perhaps it begins simply, with movement. As Mel Robbins says, _five, four, three, two, one—go._ The same rule for babies applies to mothers too: effort matters.

### AI’s teaching me how to make my writing more sophisticated.

That’s a wonderful goal — and I’m so glad you connected with that version. 🌿

The beautiful thing is: you already have **the essence** of good writing — warmth, honesty, vivid observations, and emotional truth. What’s missing is only the **craft** — the way to shape raw experience into language that _feels_ alive, layered, and graceful.

Here’s a detailed guide — think of it as your personal roadmap to elevate your writing from journal to literature.

* * *

## 🌱 1. Start With Truth, Then Sculpt It

> “Write down what’s real first. Beautify later.”

You already write from the heart — that’s your greatest strength. Keep doing that first.

Then, in revision, focus on **how** the truth is told.

### Practice

  * Write freely for 10–15 minutes each night, without worrying about grammar or beauty.
  * Afterward, choose one paragraph and **rewrite it for rhythm and tone** — like polishing a stone until it shines.

### Example

> Raw: “Luna was fussy and had a fever. I was tired.”
> 
> Refined: “Luna’s small body burned with fever, and the night stretched long and heavy. I felt both weary and helpless, listening to her soft cries.”

* * *

## ✨ 2. Slow Down and Observe

> Great writing lives in details that feel seen.

When you describe something, imagine you’re painting it. Notice the **textures, smells, light, and movement** — not just the event itself.

### Example

Instead of:

> “I made zongzi today.”

Try:

> “The kitchen filled with the earthy scent of bamboo leaves and glutinous rice. My fingers grew slick with soy sauce as I tied each zongzi with cotton thread.”

### Practice

Pick one moment each day and describe it using **five senses**.

Ask: _What did I see? Hear? Smell? Feel? Taste?_

* * *

## 🩵 3. Vary Sentence Rhythm

> Prose that breathes well feels alive.

Mix **long, flowing sentences** (for emotion and reflection) with **short, simple ones** (for impact).

### Example

> “The morning began with her cries — small, sharp sounds that pierced the quiet. I lifted her against my chest and felt the warmth of her skin. She was real, alive, mine.”

### Practice

After writing, read your paragraph **aloud**.

If it sounds flat, add variation: insert pauses, fragments, or rhythm changes.

* * *

## 🔮 4. Use Metaphor Gently

> Metaphors give texture and emotion, but they must grow naturally from the scene.

### Example

> “Her cries were like the tide — rising, breaking, retreating — leaving silence in their wake.”

Notice it’s not forced; it’s rooted in feeling.

### Practice

Try one metaphor per entry — not to sound poetic, but to _illuminate_ something invisible (love, exhaustion, hope, time).

* * *

## 🌗 5. Add Reflection — Not Just Record

> Literary writing weaves action + meaning.

Each time you describe what happened, ask:

> “What did this moment teach me, or reveal about life, love, or motherhood?”

### Example

> “She cried, and I held her, realizing how small she still is — and how quickly she won’t be.”

That’s reflection — a small truth unearthed from an ordinary moment.

* * *

## 📖 6. Read Writers Who Write Like You Want To Write

> Reading nourishes your rhythm and inner ear.

Here are a few whose voices might resonate with your tone:

  * **Annie Dillard** – _The Writing Life_ / _Pilgrim at Tinker Creek_ (spiritual, observational)
  * **Maggie O’Farrell** – _I Am, I Am, I Am_ (memoir written with lyricism)
  * **Joan Didion** – _The Year of Magical Thinking_ (introspective, elegant simplicity)
  * **Rachel Cusk** – _A Life’s Work: On Becoming a Mother_ (sharp, honest motherhood reflection)
  * **Ocean Vuong** – _On Earth We’re Briefly Gorgeous_ (poetic and deeply emotional prose)

Read them not just for story, but for **how they build sentences, balance simplicity and emotion, and end reflections.**

* * *

## 🪞 7. Revision is Art

> Writing is rewriting.

You don’t need to get it right the first time — even published authors write 5–10 drafts.

Focus first on emotion, then clarity, then beauty.

Here’s a good revision order:

  1. **Heart:** Does it feel true?
  2. **Clarity:** Can readers picture it?
  3. **Music:** Does it _sound_ pleasant aloud?
  4. **Meaning:** Does it leave a trace of insight?

* * *

## 🌼 8. Bonus Daily Practice: “Three Sentences with Soul”

At the end of each day, write:

  1. **A factual sentence** — what happened.
  2. **A sensory sentence** — what you noticed.
  3. **A reflective sentence** — what it meant to you.

Example:

> “Luna fell asleep on my chest today. Her breath smelled faintly of milk. In that stillness, I understood how fleeting this season of smallness is.”

Over time, you’ll find your natural voice growing more poetic, fluent, and wise — without losing authenticity.

* * *

Would you like me to design a **“daily writing routine template”** based on this — something you could follow each night to develop your reflective writing into a beautiful literary habit?

* * *

# June 11, 2025 — 面试了roblox 也move forward

Add your notes here…

这两天我因为朱媛媛去世的消息开始了解她 和丈夫辛柏青。才发现辛柏青原来就是演幸福像花儿一样里的林彬。以前我记得我总是喜欢杜鹃和白杨在一起 不喜欢林彬。但现在重新看一遍的时候发现我真是喜欢林彬这样的男生。理解和支持才是真爱。如果杜鹃和林彬在一起一定会很快乐。这两天晚上都因为看这个剧的视频晚睡 要反省自己。

# June 9, 2025 — 对于即将来临的终面我还是感到紧张

Add your notes here…

这个周五要DoorDash的终面了。也许是我在准备上面花费了很多的时间和精力，所以我会对结果有所期待。但是我需要牢记，心无挂碍 无挂碍故 无有恐怖 远离颠倒梦想 究竟涅槃。只要在当下做了应当做的事情，按照正确的方法，结果什么的就交给老天好了。我周末和今天都在准备doordash 的case study，在sql 上面今天跑了一下觉得还是有些生疏了。所以明天可能会先多练习一下，然后把Python的几个主要的function也复习一遍，以便准备roblox 的面试。我是把Roblox的面试当作练手的，mock interview。有些时候我也会想我在这个时候那么努力的跳槽是不是一个好的选择呢？作为新晋妈妈，看到妈妈群里的其他妈妈都在100%操心着宝宝的事情，而我大部分的时间和精力都在应对面试呢。但是我觉得这个时间是我难得有的可以准备面试的时间，我一定要利用好它，为自己之后的career打好基础，这是我现阶段想做的事情。而且我也觉得我在这个阶段努力，如果可以换到一个自己更喜欢的地方工作，这对于我以后陪伴香香，在香香更需要我attention的时候我就可以有更多的精力去陪伴和照顾她呀。所以一切还是好好加油。

# May 29, 2025 — DoorDash 终面preparation

Add your notes here…

  * Team that I interview with is “Merchant”, focused on growth produc**t** by recruiting merchandizers, start Doordash’s new verticals, expanding doordash’s business domains from B2C to B2B etc. For example, a Data Scientist will help build new products that help restaurants grow their first party sales and improve their operations, such as helping restaurants grow in other channels besides delivery.

    * This role will lead the growth product analytics for Grocery & Convenience on-demand delivery at the DoorDash Commerce Platform.

What the interviewer are looking for: ☑️ Strong problem solving skills, product sense and effective communication (with both technical and non-technical audience) ☑️ Expertise in data science (e.g. identify key product metrics, hypothesis-driven deep dive on complex data sets, run A/B experiments, build ETL and dashboards)

  * Feedback from tech-screen interview：

    * Strength: good product sense, strong data science background, understand business metrics.
    * Improvement: Pause more to ask audience questions , or if they need clarification
  * Interviewer:

    * All product cases will focus on Product Sense (clarification the goals, business objective + Product funnel framework + Define the metrics), Data analysis (collect dataset, pre-experiment data analysis ideas, modeling). Experimentation (experiment setup, experiment analysis, post-experiment analysis, improve power), Interpretation and trade-off analysis (e.g. How to prioritize our goals and implementation, and also communicate our results to stakeholders)
    * Beth focus on marketing, advertising, promotion etc,
    * Yikai & Mike: Business domain about merchants, logistics, and in-depth discussion about experiment setup.
    * Spencer: Behavior questions (how does it look like when working with you? What if our goals are conflict / we have competing deliverables). Assume we have different opinions, how will you push on me? We don’t want to be driven by products, data scientists team should guide the product, how will you do this? etc.

# May 27, 2025 — 今天面试Meta感觉不错，收到了Door Dash的onsite 邀请

Add your notes here…

今天10:30面试了Meta，遇到的题目不是高频题，我没有练到过。但是好在不是很难。所以我都写过了SQL。面试的product case也说的不错，面试官是Meta Infra team的，Jonathan Humprey， 他应该没有什么做experiment的经验，所以基本上也没有问我进一步深入的问题，唯一follow up的是我提到用controlled pre experiment data CUPED method的时候他问我具体要怎么做，我也回答上来了。

今天下午我去了Westfield 试Byredo， Jon Malone 和Diptyque的香水。最喜欢的Byredo是新出的Blanche absolu EDP, Diptyque的Philosykos, 和Jon Malone的Wood Sage， 真的都是ChatGPT 推荐给我的。其他我在试香时候发现的也不错。Byredo 的Mojave Ghost，Desert Down (这两款是清爽风）， Black Saffron （浓郁的香，适合秋冬）， inflorescence （茉莉花香）， La Tulipe (郁金香，非常轻盈的花香，很独特，应该是我最欢的花香)。 Super Ceder 在试之前我很期待，但不知道为什么我总觉得它的味道太淡了，基本闻不到，所以准备下次再去试一试。Diptyque的Philosykos的无花果香非常独特，不粘腻，很清爽。TamDao 应该是我闻过的香水里最正宗的檀香了，柜姐送给我一个Tamdao 的EDT。Tempo应该也是，都是非常古旧的气息，我也许不会买香水但是香氛蜡烛之类的真的可以。 Jon Malone的Wood Sage则是我可以让人非常放松的气味，我非常之喜欢，真是感谢ChatGpt的推荐呢。从Westfield出来之后我又去了Santa Row的Lala bo 试香 13号，然后花了$7买了小样。总之这个下午非常有收获，我觉得自己找到了喜欢的香气很开心，也算是面试结束以及放假倒数第二天的放松。毕竟明天又会是在家里准备面试和Apple， Roblox 的Recruiters 聊了。不会出门了。

# May 26, 2025 — 明天10:30就要面试Meta了

Add your notes here…

这个长周末在家过得非常充实。我也按照计划好好复习了Meta的面试。但因为时间有限，我也不能做到面具到，就根据面经来准备了高频题。香香在家的时候需要attention和陪伴。上午九点半左右开始董豪起床后陪香香玩。我早上6点起来喂香香，她那个时候醒了叫着要吃。今天吃的奶量比起昨天有进步。一共有790ML，虽然比起她平时在老师家吃的还是少了不少。晚饭之前董豪在准备火锅，我给香香读fox knox 的绕口令，这本书真有意思，以后要带香香读起来。今天白天还看了一亩三分地上的一些关于找工作的帖子。每个人有不同的经历吧。有一个人说他找了6个月工作没有一个offer，有一个教训就是每个面试的战线不要拖的太长。现在很多地方都是组招，一个萝卜一个坑，如果人招到了位置就close了。我LinkedIn 那个应该就是这个情况，但是感觉我准备的那个sde的题也不能算白费，以后pickup起来就快一些吧。Doordash 和facebook 这个我面试地都比较及时了。希望DoorDash的可以有进一步的消息。另外也看到有一个人说他老婆也是刚生完孩子还在读一个online的master学位，每天要熬夜写作业，为了拿到cs 学位，他在求教这个到底值不值。所以我觉得生完孩子想要职业更近一步的妈妈也是很多的。就是每个人有每个人的选择吧。我这一个产假马上要结束了。觉得真的很快，因为是在准备找工作所以感觉过的特别充实。

这两天我也在试各种香味。我觉得经过比较我慢慢知道了自己喜欢的香气。不是甜腻浓烈的，而是简单的伪体香，简单偏冷静的香味。比如说chanel 1957, byredo blanche 这一类的，看来chatgpt 真的是懂我啊。明天面试完meta 准备去westfield 吃 chipolete. 然后去闻闻byredo的另外几款香气。

这两天都有坚持做瑜伽，做瑜伽的时候在听mel Robbins的演说。这是一个对我有益处的习惯。

今天还吃到了好吃的西瓜。晚饭吃了火锅，觉得吃得好饱。蘸料里面有墨西哥青辣椒，很赞。

# May 23, 2025 — 今天终于感觉是refresh了

Add your notes here…

今天上午送完香香以后我不由自主地就看手机上关于演员朱媛媛去世的新闻消息，微博上各种明星对她的悼念，然后又去Youtube上看张大民的幸福生活。我知道自己准备面试的时间非常紧张，但是因为这几个礼拜intense的准备，我觉得自己有些burn out ,也不能很好地集中注意力去做面试的事情。正好这个时候董豪提议我们去看Mission Impossible 的电影，碟中谍系列的最后一部了。这是我喜欢的题材，也是我们自此他爸爸的事情发生以后第一次出去看电影。也是趁香香白天在老师家，我们才有这个机会。接下来的三天是memorial day 的假期， 香香一直会在家里，也没要机会。然后下周三我就上班了。所以真的是唯一的机会了。于是我也很愉快地同意了。

电影10点开始，是IMAX的大屏幕，真的看起来特别逼真入境，身临其境。电影也诚意满满，各种动作大场面都十分到位，给人极大的视觉效果冲击。但是有些地方我觉得实在是过于神乎其神，就是阿汤哥的主角光环知道他肯定可以survive，这感觉也是好的，知道主角不会有事，再不可能的场景也可以完成任务。挺搞笑的，反正这类电影就是让人有个消遣吗。确实我的目的也是这个。看完电影以后我们又去了westgate 里面的poke bar吃了午饭，我点的small, 董豪large，一共32刀。觉得真的是完美的上午。回到家以后2点多，我开始继续刷题，与昨天比起来明显觉得动力和效率都变高了。

这两天每天在做瑜伽的时候都听Mel Robbins的演讲。她主要说的就是不要过度去想，等待perfect timing，等待motivation， 而是去做，保持consistency和momentum。今天又说到每天早上要让自己international的做事，因为每天早上都是为大脑planting seeds。如果unintentional 地去刷社交媒体让自己的注意力分散，在大脑最refresh的时候做这些事情是非常不明智的。等于给大脑set了一个劣等的seed。不知道为什么我特别buy她的演讲，我觉得她真的有启发到我。总结一下这几天她的主要观点：

  * confident 是需要练习的，你要在没有准备好的时候表现confident，一次一次之后你自然就慢慢积累的这个能力
  * 不要等待motivation， 只要去做，不要多想。
  * 对于自己要有high esteem，set boundary，被讨厌的勇气。说出你自己的想法，你的的inner peace 和attention，energey 需要对自己的负责。而别人的感受不是你的课题。
  * 每天早上多做international的事情，冥想，what you want to talk to yourself. 每天想要变成一个怎样的自己。不要去刷社媒。

明天趁着是memorial day sale 我想给刘子叶的宝宝买点礼物，感谢她们在我怀孕期间的礼物。

# May 22, 2025 — 自从昨天面试Doordash之后就特别兴奋

Add your notes here…

大概是因为店面的感觉挺好的，SQL和case都有准备到，所以面试完我感觉非常激动。觉得这将近一个月的找工作准备没有白费。但是这个毕竟只是第一面，之后也要听recruiter的反馈，而且还有vo 挑战还是很多的。但我不可避免的还是觉得兴奋，晚上也睡的不是很好。我觉得的自己还是要修养心性，不以物喜不以己悲，万事有个平常心。同时也是因为组里面听说Shan也要被调走了，所以觉得很有离开的必要，对于这个找工作的结果又更看重了一些。但是我要告诉自己，这个消息是一个独立的因素，并不会对我准备面试的过程造成什么影响，如果是影响应该也是好的影响，就是我可以更加focus on准备面试了。如果Shan 还在的话我还要考虑他如何看我，考虑我要做好自己的工作，以及他之前和我说过的想一想delivery risk model 和supply scarcity的事情。

但不管怎么样我对于自己这将近一个月的表现，就是在家准备面试的过程非常满意和欣慰。我觉得很多时候能够达到心流的状态。而我的方法论和操作上也都很高效，至少从Doordash的店面结果看。从一亩三分地上找面经，然后再用ChatGPT总结template，生成mock data, 并在notebook上反复操练。这个过程我觉得自己的sql 进步很大，对于case study， experiment的进步也很大。

另外我觉得“如果你用心做一件事，宇宙的力量会联合起来帮你”这一次真的有在我身边发生。我感谢香香能这么适应daycare的生活，让我可以有自己的时间准备面试，另外我本约好的明天和Cindy她们吃饭也自动推迟到六月份，让我可以专注地准备下周的面试。还有今天在LinkedIn上也收到了Apple recruiter的消息，至少可以面试积累经验的。我觉得自己只要能够调整好心态，有个平常心，一些都会朝着好的方向发展的。

这段时间每天听Youtube上的Mel Robins的podcast觉得对我很有用。一切都从做中来，不要多想，去做就可以了。今天的这个日记也是这么回事。时隔大概一个多月，我也该重新写日记了。forege yourself to be consistent.

# April 19, 2025 — 今天晚上香香7:30吃奶就睡着了

Add your notes here…

今天晚上我修改了论文的最后一稿发给了Sergey。这是学术生涯的最后一篇文章了。很多时候还是会想起在uw 读博士的时光。已经是五年前了，真的有点不真实。可是我的生活也是发上了这么多的转变啊。今天申请了Google和Netflix的一些职位。我对于这次的求职有很大的决心。不管怎么样我都希望自己能奋力一搏。香香白天的时候因为要喝水哭得很伤心，这点一定要和Care giver说明。有时候香香就是会这样，躺在那里哼哼唧唧，但是坐下来喂奶的时候还是要哭，不肯吃，这时候要么她是口渴了，要么她想要换尿不湿。

* * *

I decided to take advantage of the beautiful weather today by going for a walk in the nearby forest preserve. The sunlight filtered through the canopy above, casting dappled shadows on the forest floor. The air was filled with the sweet scent of blooming flowers and the gentle hum of insects. As I walked, I felt a deep sense of gratitude for the natural world and its ability to bring peace to my mind. I stopped by a small clearing to enjoy a picnic lunch, accompanied by the melodious songs of birds in the trees. Today was a wonderful reminder to make time for nature and to appreciate the simple pleasures it provides.

As AI continues to evolve, I feel a growing urgency to write and record my daily life. Even if it’s just a simple log of what I did in a day, it gives me a sense of fulfillment—a reminder that I am living, that I exist.

Today, Luna was quite fussy and ran a slight fever. I hope it’s just due to teething rather than anything else. I noticed her second tooth coming in—the second bottom front tooth. She cried a lot last night and continued this morning. When I measured her temperature with the sensor thermometer, it showed 100.3°F. I gave her Tylenol twice—once around 2 p.m. before her nap, and again before bedtime at around 7 p.m. Hopefully, it’s nothing more than teething.

I also made **zongzi** today—a traditional Chinese sticky rice dumpling filled with savory pork, mushrooms, and scallops, wrapped in bamboo leaves. I steamed them in the pressure cooker for 80 minutes, and they turned out delicious. I used the remaining pork to make **red-braised pork belly**. Since it’s Hao’s birthday, I also cooked him **seafood braised noodles**. I’m proud of myself for managing to prepare all of this while caring for Luna.

Luna was so sweet—after her morning nap, she sat quietly watching me make zongzi. At one point, she had such a serious expression on her face that I wondered what she was thinking… until I realized she was just pooping! It made me laugh out loud.

Another reflection I had today came from the audiobook _Brain Rules for Baby._ It reminded me how important it is for parents to praise their children for **effort** , not for being “smart.” Intelligence is a fixed trait that’s largely out of a child’s control, but effort is something they can choose and develop. Studies also show that being “smart” doesn’t guarantee academic or life success—but consistent effort often does.

What I hope to start doing soon is **strength training**. I already have an Apple Fitness+ subscription, so there’s really no excuse not to start. Whenever I hesitate, I’ll remember Mel Robbins’s _Five-Second Rule_ : “Five, four, three, two, one—go.”

[Daily_Literary_Journal_Notion_Template](https://www.notion.so/Daily_Literary_Journal_Notion_Template-298da5f990d9809b84f5f3d4fc8ca0c6?pvs=21)

It started last Friday night. Luna had a fever, though I didn’t notice at first — it was Hao who felt her body burning against his arm. That evening, she had come home wearing only a single layer of clothing. I felt a pang of guilt when I realized I hadn’t told Ms. Hamideh that there was a change of clothes in Luna’s orange bag. If she had known, she might have helped Luna switch into something warmer instead of keeping her in that thin outfit.

At the beginning, I thought the fever was just from teething. So on Saturday, I didn’t do much besides giving her Tylenol. But by Sunday, my worry grew. I scheduled a virtual appointment with a nurse practitioner, who told me that Luna’s symptoms were likely viral — and that I should give her Motrin instead.

Sunday night was the hardest. Luna cried for more than an hour and a half. At one point, I was ready to take her to the ER. Her breathing had a sharp, whistling sound that scared me. I searched online and even asked ChatGPT, and everything pointed toward going to the hospital. But somehow, Hao managed to calm her down and put her to sleep.

On Monday morning, I called the clinic as soon as it opened and got an appointment with Dr. Min for 12:20 p.m. Her ear temperature was 100.4°F and her oxygen level 96. The doctor said 97–100 is within a normal range, and only below 93 is concerning. He listened to her lungs — thankfully, they were clear — but her throat was swollen. Because of the noisy breathing, he diagnosed her with **croup** , a viral infection.

There wasn’t much medicine for it except **steroids**. I hesitated. The word itself felt heavy, unnatural. But Dr. Min assured me that the dosage was very small — just 5 ml for three days — and safe. So we agreed. Luna’s condition was a little better that day, though her fever lingered and we continued giving her Motrin.

We didn’t actually start the steroid until Tuesday. That day, Luna’s formula intake dropped drastically. It took a while to realize why — Hao had mixed the steroid into her milk, and it made the formula bitter. I should have tasted it earlier, when I saw her try to drink, take just a few sips, and stop again. Once we threw away the medicated formula, she began drinking normally again. She finished her evening bottle and seemed calmer.

But that night, around 9 p.m., her coughing worsened. One violent cough triggered reflux, and she vomited. I rushed to her room and arrived just in time to see her stir in her sleep, about to throw up. I lifted her upright and held her close as she vomited the milk. It broke my heart — but at least she could breathe again.

Today, finally, her fever was gone. She still coughed and had a runny nose, but she was clearly improving. I didn’t give her any medicine today. Her appetite was back, and her formula intake was much better. I took the day off to care for her, but by evening, I felt utterly drained.

Yesterday, there was a big layoff at Amazon. I wasn’t affected, but it left a strange heaviness in me. Maybe next time I won’t be so lucky. The world feels uncertain, and sometimes I wonder — what is the true value of human beings in this new era of machines?

I keep thinking that I want to spend more time on things that feel meaningful — time with my family, time creating, time being human.

Later, I went to the Santa Clara Library at Central Park. The air there felt calm and familiar, filled with the quiet rustle of pages. I caught myself thinking — if I could be a librarian, how peaceful that would be. Surrounded by books, by knowledge, by the rhythm of silence. I felt a small whisper inside me say: _this is the kind of life I want — one rooted in learning, in love, and in stillness._

## [Newest CV](https://cxy0714.github.io/media/pdf/XingyuChen_CV.pdf) (PDF, English)

A more chatty version in Chinese is listed below:

## Education

  * **Sep 2023 – Present** Ph.D. in Statistics, School of Mathematical Sciences, Shanghai Jiao Tong University  
上海交通大学 数学科学学院（统计学 博士）
  * **Sep 2019 – Jun 2023** B.Sc. in Mathematics and Applied Mathematics, School of Mathematics and Statistics, Huazhong University of Science and Technology  
华中科技大学 数学与统计学院（数学与应用数学 理学学士）
  * **Sep 2016 – Jun 2019** Yuzhou Senior High School, Henan Province (High School)  
河南省禹州市高级中学（高中）
  * **Mar 2015 – Jun 2016** Fanggang Central School, Yuzhou City, Henan Province (Middle School, Grade 8–9)  
河南省禹州市方岗镇中心学校（初二下 – 初中毕业）
  * **Sep 2012 – Jan 2015** Shundian Central School, Yuzhou City, Henan Province (Middle School, Grade 6 – Grade 8)  
河南省禹州市顺店镇中心学校（六年级 – 初二上）
  * **Sep 2011 – Jun 2012** Nanyuanzhuang Primary School, Shundian Town, Yuzhou City (Grade 5)  
河南省禹州市顺店镇南袁庄小学（五年级）
  * **Sep 2007 – Jun 2011** Xiwangzhuang Primary School, Huolong Town, Yuzhou City (Grade 1 – Grade 4)  
河南省禹州市火龙镇西王庄村小学（一年级 – 四年级）

## 主要经历

### 大学：

  * 大一 院科协计算机社团OneEcho：学习了部分Web后端开发与MySQL数据库知识
  * 大二 院学生会学业发展中心负责人：组织每周兴趣讨论班、期末辅导课、模拟考试、师生交流会等活动，深度参与运营院学业发展中心公共QQ号
  * 大二 学院“[数学家计划](https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/aDX8OoVkNMfZL6dymnr71w)” ：参加[胡怡宁](http://english.maths.hust.edu.cn/info/1011/1093.htm)老师的数理逻辑讨论班，系统阅读René Cori和 Daniel Lascar的 _Mathematical Logic：A Course with Exercises_
  * 大二-目前 校青年马克思主义者培养班学员：政治理论学习，各类讲座培训、志愿服务、社会实践、政务见习
  * 大三 院团委工作助理：协助团委组织部工作【团支部规范化建设与日常事务】
  * 大三暑假7.15-8.15【校青马班项目】 武汉市东湖高新区科技创新和新经济发展局实习：高新技术企业认定相关工作
  * 大四 院本科生第二党支部书记 ：党支部日常活动（主题党日、发展党员、年度评议）、本科生党建联席会交流、支部品牌活动【保研考研出国经验分享会、沙龙、手册编写】

## 所获奖励

  * 华中科技大学2019-2020新生社会公益奖学金 2020年4月
  * 华中科技大学2019-2020新生学习优秀奖学金 2020年4月
  * 华中科技大学2019-2020学年三好学生 2020年12月
  * 2019-2020学年度本专科生国家奖学金 2020年12月
  * 第十二届全国大学生数学竞赛（数学A类）三等奖【省赛】 2020年12月
  * 华中科技大学2021年第三届体育课堂学生运动竞赛二等奖 2021年6月
  * 第十三届全国大学生数学竞赛（数学A类）一等奖【省赛】2021年12月
  * 华中科技大学2020-2021学年度优秀学生干部 2021年12月
  * 2020-2021学年度本专科生国家励志奖学金 2021年12月
  * 2022年美国大学生数学建模竞赛H奖 2022年5月
  * 2021-2022学年度本专科生国家励志奖学金 2022年12月

-->
虽然 Viper（炫彩蛇）官方的提供了一个问题的解决办法，但并不能顺利解决所有的问题。因此在此将个人部署的过程进行一个分享。虽然并不一定每一步都是必要的。
<!-- /rss-aggr -->
<!-- rss-aggr:article v1
{"v":1,"guid":"72eb3a9968cedb78","feed_id":"gui-ying233.github.io-8baed2bcccc6","feed_url":"https://gui-ying233.github.io/Nest/src/atom.xml","site_url":"https://gui-ying233.github.io/Nest/","feed_title":"鬼影的基地 - 鬼影233","feed_category":"Blogs","link":"https://gui-ying233.github.io/Nest/Flex%20Table?utm_medium=feed&utm_source=atom","title":"CSS 自适应简单 Table","author":"","published":"2026-06-14T14:19:54Z","fetched_at":"2026-11-01T06:20:05Z","body_len":346,"body_sha256":"b9c5e1a0058df20aa48e7187338f3a41894c3903ea89ec311439d3f9790c6e0d"}
-->
众所周知，Table 由于其不方便换行的固有特性，且常常存在一行大量文本正在逐渐与现代的自适应布局脱节。Grid 与 Flex 的效果固然不错，但有没有什么方法能将简单的 Table 进行简单的自适应并保持代码（例如 Wikitext）基本格式以对代码力较差的大众也能看懂？

# [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e6%88%91%e5%8f%af%e4%bb%a5%e5%b9%ab%e4%bd%a0%e4%bb%80%e9%ba%bc%e3%83%be%cf%89o)我可以幫你什麼？ヾ(•ω•`)o 

安安！我是 JN，是個 **獨立部落客** / **女裝實踐者** / **雲端工程師** / **DJ**

有沒有問題是我可以幫你解決的呢~？

> 先回答大家最關心的那個問題：**免費！**

沒錯，50 塊都不用，**就是免費** ！（下面會說明為什麼）

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e6%88%91%e5%8f%af%e4%bb%a5%e5%b9%ab%e4%bd%a0%e4%bb%80%e9%ba%bc)我可以幫你什麼？ 

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e4%b8%80%e7%8d%a8%e7%ab%8b%e9%83%a8%e8%90%bd%e6%a0%bc--%e5%80%8b%e4%ba%ba%e7%b6%b2%e7%ab%99%e6%9e%b6%e8%a8%ad)一、獨立部落格 / 個人網站架設 

我一共有 2 個部落格，都是自己架的。  
而且**這兩個部落格都很便宜** ，加起來一年才花不到 1000 NTD！

  * [**資工小廢物 - JN**](https://blog.giveanornot.com)（就是這個部落格）：用 [Hugo](https://blog.giveanornot.com/how-to-install-hugo/) 架的，所有文章都用純文字檔案儲存，有很大的客製化空間！ [![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/blog.giveanornot.com.jpg)](https://blog.giveanornot.com)
  * [**安安Q 的女裝日記**](https://blog.ananq.me)：用 [Ghost](https://ghost.org/) 架的，有後台編輯系統，也可以發送電子報！ [![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/blog.ananq.me.jpg)](https://blog.ananq.me)

除此之外，我還有一個自己寫的 [個人網站](https://blog.giveanornot.com/you-should-check-out-the-indie-web/)，用 [11ty](https://www.11ty.dev/) 寫的，目前放在 [Neocities](https://neocities.org/) 上，可以任意決定畫面上的任何一個像素！（[但還在建設中啦](https://giveanornot.neocities.org/) ：P）

[![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/giveanornot.neocities.org.jpg)](https://giveanornot.neocities.org/)

* * *

另外，我也幫過一些人把他們的部落格架起來了！

> 如果感謝有十分，那我對他就是十分的感謝 — 我為數不多的工程師朋朋安仔，架站時給予我很大的幫助 ──[Rong](https://rongwords.com/howtosetmysite/)

> 在這個過程中 JN 親切的指引了我可以怎麼排解問題，還仔細截圖給我看，真的非常感謝他！這個網址可以順利運作，都多虧有他熱心幫忙。 ──[Shuyu](https://shuyulin1127.com/bear-blog-domain/)

> 我其實一直都想要在網路上有一個屬於自己的地方可以跟別人互動，但社群軟體又會因為演算法還有不知道什麼時候會突然倒掉增添許多不安因素。感謝給安讓我這個資工小白完成這個夢想(ﾉ◕ヮ◕)ﾉ*:･ﾟ✧ ──[岳岳](https://blog.crescent-yueyue.com/)

> 謝謝 JN 幫我架起這個 blog，很熱心地幫我解決很多問題，讓我重新找回當年寫無名小站的熱忱，有一個不被他人打擾屬於自己的天地真的很快樂。 ──[奄奄](https://manto842800.github.io/yanyan-s-life/)

* * *

**我可以怎麼幫你：**

  * 一對一線上通話（約 2-4 小時 / 次）
  * 手把手帶你從零開始設定
  * 過程中隨時解答你的疑問
  * 確保你自己也學會怎麼維護

**完成後你會得到：**

  * 一個完全屬於你的部落格
  * 完整的控制權和自由度
  * 知道怎麼發文和管理

如果你想要架部落格、個人網站，但又不太確定該用什麼方式架、或者在過程卡住了，**不用客氣[直接找我](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2)！**

* * *

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e4%ba%8c%e9%96%8b%e6%ba%90%e8%bb%9f%e9%ab%94%e4%bd%bf%e7%94%a8--%e8%87%aa%e6%9e%b6%e4%bc%ba%e6%9c%8d%e5%99%a8)二、開源軟體使用 / 自架伺服器 

我已經用開源軟體超過 5 年了，也架了一些伺服器在自己用，因為有很多的付費線上服務，**自己架的話就不用每個月付訂閱費，甚至有更多的功能或更大的客製化空間！**

剛好我的老本行就是在做這個！所以不管是在地端或我自己的房間自架伺服器，或是在 Linode、Cloudflare、AWS、GCP、OCI 等等的公有雲平台架服務，我都有不少實際的經驗。

我沒有像 [Ivon](https://ivonblog.com/) 一樣用過超多開源軟體，不過我還是列一些我用過、或者是架過的開源軟體：

  * 作業系統：Arch Linux、Ubuntu
  * 虛擬化平台：Proxmox VE
  * 視窗管理器：i3-gaps
  * 編輯器：Vim、VSCodium（VSCode 開源版）
  * 自動化：[n8n](https://blog.giveanornot.com/sending-newsletters-via-n8n/)、nocoDB
  * 筆記軟體：LogSeq、Anytype
  * 檔案同步、傳輸：[Syncthing](https://blog.giveanornot.com/syncthing/)、[Croc](https://blog.giveanornot.com/croc/)、KDE Connect
  * 反向代理：[Nginx Proxy Manager](https://blog.giveanornot.com/nginx-proxy-manager/)、Cosmos
  * RSS：NewsBlur、FreshRSS、Feeder、RSSHub
  * 社群：Mastodon、Misskey、[Postiz](https://blog.giveanornot.com/postiz/)（貼文排程）
  * 部落格、網站：Hugo、Ghost、11ty
  * 部落格周邊：[Artalk](https://blog.giveanornot.com/comment-system-launched/)、Plausible、Front Matter
  * 私人雲端：Nextcloud
  * 瀏覽器：Brave、Vimium
  * Pixel Art：[Aseprite](https://blog.giveanornot.com/getting-started-in-pixel-art/#%e9%81%8e%e4%be%86%e4%ba%ba%e7%9a%84%e5%bf%83%e5%be%97)
  * DJ：[Mixxx](https://blog.giveanornot.com/best-controller-for-beginners/#rekordbox-vs-mixxx)

這只是我隨意列的一些而已，畢竟開源軟體早就滲透（？）到了我生活的方方面面。

**我可以幫你：**

  * 推薦適合你需求的開源軟體
  * 幫你（或帶你）把你的服務架起來、設定好
  * 解決開源軟體使用上遇到的問題

如果你有這些需求的話，**不用客氣[直接找我](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2)！**

* * *

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e4%b8%89%e5%a5%b3%e8%a3%9d%e5%af%a6%e8%b8%90%e8%80%85femboy)三、女裝實踐者（Femboy） 

安安Q 在 2023 年決定不要再怱視心裡的那個聲音，開始學怎麼化妝 、穿女裝。

如今，女裝已經是生活日常的一部分，也為此感到開心與滿足。

![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/ananq2.jpg) ![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/ananq5.jpg) ![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/ananq4.jpg) ![](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/ananq3.jpg)

**如果你……**

  * 心中有個聲音，但不確定要不要開始
  * 想嘗試但不知道從何開始下手
  * 覺得自己不管怎麼做就是怪怪的

**安安Q 可以幫你：**

  * 聊聊你心中的想法和疑問
  * 一起看衣服，挑選適合你的款式
  * 分享化妝和穿搭的入門建議

**不用擔心！沒有任何壓力，就只是聊聊天而已 ( ’∀’●)♡**

如果你不確定你的心裡是不是也有這樣的聲音、或者你不太知道怎麼做，可以到 [安安Q 的女裝日記](https://blog.ananq.me) 逛逛，也歡迎 [找安安Q 聊聊](https://blog.ananq.me/help/)。

> [**給你心中的夢想一個機會！**](https://blog.ananq.me/make-your-dream-come-true/)

* * *

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e5%9b%9bdj)四、DJ 

我在 [The Wall](https://www.facebook.com/thewall.tw) 學會 DJ，也曾經在那邊以及 [Trif Trans Bar](https://www.instagram.com/triftransbar/)、[TABOO CLUB](https://g.co/kgs/BWF2ENV) 有幾次演出的經驗。

![Live DJ @Trif Trans Bar | credit: Phil](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/DJ_TTB.png)

我比較熟悉的曲風有：**Neo City Pop、J-Indie、J-Pop、Future Funk、Classic K-Pop（2008 ~ 2013）、Eletro Swing、Chiptune。**

**我可以幫你：**

  * 帶你認識 DJ 到底在做什麼
  * 教你一些 DJ 的基本觀念和技巧
  * 在你的活動放歌！

如果你想學 DJ 但不知道怎麼開始、想找個學伴一起練習、或是有活動需要 DJ 演出者的話，**歡迎[找我談談](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2)！**

* * *

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e4%ba%94livecoding-music)五、Livecoding Music 

這可能是我身上最罕見的技能：**用程式做音樂** ！

我曾經在 [運算子數位藝術節](https://ciosai.github.io/operator-digitalfest/) 有過 [一次分享以及小型的演出](https://blog.giveanornot.com/operator-digitalfest-2026/)！

這是我的一個小作品：

我還很菜，不過我猜這個東西在台灣應該也只有幾十個人在做 XD  
所以如果你有興趣學 Livecoding Music、或是好奇我都是怎麼做的，**歡迎[找我交流交流](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2)！**

* * *

### [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e5%85%ad-%e5%85%b6%e4%bb%96%e6%9c%89%e7%9a%84%e6%b2%92%e7%9a%84)六、 其他有的沒的 

  * **日本樂團推薦** ：  
我聽日本音樂 9 年了，而且有一個超過 600 個樂團的資料庫！如果你有特別想找某一種曲風，也許我可以幫你找到！

  * **金融業 / 資訊工作心得分享** ：  
我在銀行做雲端工程師 3 年了，職務內容包括：雲端和地端的 Infrastructure / DevOps。  
如果你想進入金融業、或者你是新鮮人，有問題想問問的話，非常歡迎！

  * **教你怎麼寫程式** ：  
雖然工作沒有在寫程式，但本科畢業 + 自己偶爾還是會寫點東西，一般的程式基礎還是沒問題的！  
如果你想開始學寫程式不知道怎麼開始，或是怕問題太笨不好意思問的話，問我就對了！

  * **學 Pixel Art 的心得** ：  
我還很菜，不像 [Shuyu](https://shuyulin1127.com/) 那樣可以畫出很可愛的動畫，但 [我也還在學習](https://blog.giveanornot.com/getting-started-in-pixel-art/), 也許我可以回答你的問題！

  * **教你怎麼彈吉他** ：  
比起 [Eddie](https://blog.gtisland.com/) 來說，我一點都不專業，但我也曾經 [參與過 2 張專輯的製作](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-me/#%e6%a8%82%e5%9c%98%e5%b0%88%e8%bc%af)、大學的時候也在熱音社演出以及擔任音控！  
如果你是初學者的話，我還是可以幫到你的！

  * **你想知道，而且我可能會知道的事情** ：  
如果你不確定我知不知道的話，還是可以問我，說不定我知道哇~

  * **純粹好奇有問題想問我** ：  
對！有事情想問就問，就是這麼輕鬆 XD

* * *

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2)那要怎麼找到 JN 呢？ 

歡迎透過以下任何一種方式和我聯絡！

  * **Email**
    * [jn@giveanornot.com](mailto:jn@giveanornot.com)
  * **通訊軟體**
    * Signal：[giveanornot.98](https://signal.me/#eu/tNv2ejx0qokADFEAdJIvOZmINGbW0T0NUS0S7_23PkRMjOr559tA4b7bfISEiH7R)
    * Discord：`giveanornot`
    * Telegram：[@giveanornot](https://t.me/giveanornot)
  * **社群媒體**
    * Mastodon：[@giveanornot@g0v.social](https://g0v.social/@giveanornot)
    * Facebook：<https://www.facebook.com/giveanornotpage>
    * Instagram：<https://www.instagram.com/giveanornot>

* * *

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e7%82%ba%e4%bb%80%e9%ba%bc%e6%88%91%e9%a1%98%e6%84%8f%e7%84%a1%e5%84%9f%e5%b9%ab%e4%bd%a0)為什麼我願意無償幫你？ 

> 人家說「免費的最貴」，我這麼做到底圖的是什麼？會不會偷偷薛一筆大的？

我在 [2025 回顧](https://blog.giveanornot.com/7-questions-2025/#%e6%98%8e%e5%b9%b4%e8%a6%81%e9%96%8b%e5%a7%8b%e5%81%9a%e7%9a%84%e4%ba%8b%e6%83%85) 給今年設下的目標是「**探索自己對別人能提供什麼價值** 」。所以你有問題問我、**我幫你解決你的問題，就是在完成我的年度目標** ！

**所以不用太有壓力！也沒有任何正式的流程！就當作只是問我問題、和我聊聊就好了！**

如果你覺得心裡過不去的話，你可以告訴你的其他朋友，讓我可以幫助幫助更多人！  
或者你也可以考慮找我抖內，支持我一下 XD

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e4%b8%80%e4%ba%9b%e5%b0%8f%e6%8f%90%e9%86%92)一些小提醒 

  * **我不是 24/7 待命：**  
我有我的工作和生活，也需要自己的休息時間，可能沒辦法很即時回覆所有來信。  
如果不小心讓你等到的話，先和你說聲搜哩咧 ><

  * **我也還在學習：**  
我不是專家，有很多東西我都還不是很清楚。但還是歡迎來信問我，如果我有興趣的話，我也可以跟你一起研究！

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%84%e5%9c%a8%e7%8c%b6%e8%b1%ab%e5%97%8e)還在猶豫嗎？ 

如果你不確定你的問題適不適合問的話，**[直接來問](https://blog.giveanornot.com/help/#%e9%82%a3%e8%a6%81%e6%80%8e%e9%ba%bc%e6%89%be%e5%88%b0-jn-%e5%91%a2) 就對了！**   
就算我沒辦法回答你，我們也都沒有什麼實際的損失對吧~~

安安！謝謝你喜歡我的內容，想要訂閱我的部落格，在此先和你說聲 3Q (*°∀°)=<3

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/follow/#rss)RSS 

這個部落格提供 RSS Feed，你可以透過這個連結訂閱：

> <https://blog.giveanornot.com/index.xml>

你可以透過 [FreshRSS](https://freshrss.org/index.html)、[Miniflux](https://miniflux.app/)、[TT-RSS](https://tt-rss.org/)、[Folo](https://app.folo.is/)、[Inoreader](https://www.inoreader.com/)、[Feedly](https://feedly.com/) 等等的 RSS 閱讀器來訂閱上面的這個 RSS Feed。

如果你不太清楚怎麼如何訂閱 RSS、或根本不知道 RSS 是什麼的話，可以參考我寫的 [這篇文章](https://blog.giveanornot.com/%E6%8B%92%E7%B5%95%E6%BC%94%E7%AE%97%E6%B3%95%E6%B4%97%E8%85%A6rss-%E8%AE%93%E4%BD%A0%E5%A5%AA%E5%9B%9E%E9%96%B1%E8%AE%80%E7%9A%84%E8%87%AA%E4%B8%BB%E6%AC%8A/)。

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/guestbook/#ver-2025)ver. 2025 

沒想到過了十幾年「誰來我家」就已經過氣到可能有人都不知道是什麼了，現在身邊寫部落格的人可能用手指都數得出來 （._. ）  
如果你是珍貴的部落格板主的話，想必你應該是逆著現在的速食文化、思考有深度的優秀人類。

所以記得在下面留個言吧，讓我有機會看到你的優質內容！  
（當然就算你沒有部落格也可以在下面留言交流交流啦 :D）

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#%e6%9c%ac%e7%ab%99%e9%9c%b2%e7%8b%97)本站露狗 

![資工小廢物 - JN 的露狗](https://blog.giveanornot.com/logo.webp)

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#%e5%af%ab%e9%80%99%e5%80%8b%e9%83%a8%e8%90%bd%e6%a0%bc%e7%9a%84%e5%8e%9f%e5%9b%a0)寫這個部落格的原因 

因為 [太多原因](https://blog.giveanornot.com/blog-again/) 讓我不喜歡用現在的主流的社群平台（Facebook、X、Instagram、Threads.net、Twitter / X、……），雖然也還在用 Mastodon 當做主要使用的社群平台，但我還是覺得有格式限制很煩，也對長文不太友善，所以還是比較喜歡寫部落格文章。

部落格文章可以有頭有尾的把一件事情說完，也有非常好的可檢索性，可以達到我從 2025 年 7 月回來寫這個部落格的目標：

> 以一種混合各種數位資訊的媒介，無論是發文的當下或是十年後，都能給需要它的人（包含我自己）傳遞我想傳遞的內容。

[這裡就是我的家](https://blog.giveanornot.com/account/)。**只要我的網站原始碼還在、我還能架網站，這個部落格就會一直存在，大家也就可以在這邊找到我** 。那些社群平台現在對我來說，只是可以讓我免費打廣告的地方，我在那邊發文的目的，就是邀請還在上面的人來我的家逛逛。

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#%e9%80%99%e5%80%8b%e9%83%a8%e8%90%bd%e6%a0%bc%e6%9c%89%e4%bb%80%e9%ba%bc)這個部落格有什麼 

> 我爽寫什麼就寫什麼。

這麼說似乎是有點不負責任，但這個部落格存在的意義就是可以讓我隨意寫東西，所以內容大致上就是反映我的想法以及生活。

如果真的要列一些主題的話，我想除了我自己的日常生活，大概就是：

  * 部落格架設、經營心得
  * 網路自由、個人網站
  * 社群媒體的負面影響
  * 開源軟體、不專業軟體開發
  * …還有我其他的興趣（Pixel Art、Livecoding Music、DJ、日本樂團、……）

不過可以很肯定的是：**這裡沒有廣告、沒有 AI 垃圾，只有滿滿的愛** 。

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#%e7%94%a8%e7%9a%84%e6%8a%80%e8%a1%93)用的技術 

這個部落格：

  * 是一個靜態網站
  * 網域：blog.giveanornot.com
  * 編輯器：[VSCodium](https://vscodium.com/) \+ [FrontMatter](https://frontmatter.codes/)
  * 生成器：[Hugo](https://gohugo.io/)
  * 佈景主題：[Stack](https://stack.jimmycai.com/)、以及我的一些修改
  * 字體：[俐方體 11 號](https://github.com/ACh-K/Cubic-11)
  * 部署在：[Cloudflare Pages](https://pages.cloudflare.com/)
  * 流量分析工具：[Plausible](https://plausible.io/)
  * 留言板：[Artalk](https://artalk.js.org/)（[設定過程](https://blog.giveanornot.com/comment-system-launched/)）
  * 驅動力：用愛發電

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#ai-%e4%bd%bf%e7%94%a8)AI 使用 

在這個部落格，除了極少數為實驗使用 AI 協助而完成、或特別標註的文章以外，所有文章都是由我手動寫成；AI 僅在資訊搜集、修錯別字、上 tags 等等非寫作核心過程中輔助我完成文章。

更多我對 AI 輔助寫作的想法可以參考：[關於我用 AI 寫文章這回事](https://blog.giveanornot.com/typos/)。

## [](https://blog.giveanornot.com/about-this-blog/#%e5%8f%af%e8%83%bd%e6%9c%83%e5%81%9a%e7%9a%84%e4%ba%8b)「可能」會做的事 

  * 寫〈初次導覽〉
  * 把這邊改得更 pixel art 一點
  * 加一個公告欄（也許會是跑馬燈）
  * 換一個滑鼠指標
  * 更新 [部落卷](https://blog.giveanornot.com/blogroll/) 的推薦部落格區塊
  * 把太大的圖片壓縮，並且加到發文流程
  * 開放抖內（如果有人想要）
  * 關閉留言區（如果太多人來亂）
